## 0. What C1 is, and what kind of evidence

**Role.** One frozen plan for the method stage and the closed loop on external data (D13 Phase 4); the external run is "the same study" as K8 (D14 section 1).

**Development evidence.** K6 was a pre-registered NO-GO (power floor on T3). K7 was never frozen. K8 on the 840 nuScenes scenes is exploratory; its numbers below are from the Linux replicate and its v03 analysis, confirmed by the independent review R16 (`70_TESTS/R16_K8_RESULTS_REVIEW_v01.md`; errata E8 to E16). Decision mode: infeasible-decision rate T1 M\* 0.114, B4 0.198, B6 0.176; T3 M\* 0.161, B4 0.208, B6 0.167. On T3, Form B turns on B6: M\* − B6 = −0.005 [−0.017, +0.005] (E12).
- **Under the rules of this plan** (Choices 50, 52 and 53; development replay v02, `70_TESTS/c1_v01/status_v10/c1_devreplay_v02.json`, 57f7c91b…, B = 40000, and development check v03, B = 20000), the development verdict is the same as under every earlier rule set on both trackers: Valid(M\*) holds; V_t is {B3} on T1 and {B0, B3} on T3; Forms A, B and C are met; H10 and H3 hold. Form B's bounds are −0.722 (T1, B3, at 0.9875), −0.565 (T3, B0) and −0.677 (T3, B3, at 0.99375); Form C's −0.066 (T1) and −0.034 (T3); H10's NI −0.043 (T1) and +0.005 (T3). H3 passes at every Holm level (check v03).

**What "valid" means (E10).** "Not shown above δ" by a one-sided simultaneous test at 0.95, never "at most δ". M\*'s point estimate exceeds δ in 9 of 32 PD7 cells on T1 and 11 on T3 on development data, none near a flag.

**What the primary test adds (Choice 6; R20 P2, Choice 47f; restricted by Choice 52f).** Within-AV2 cross-fitting gives M\*'s own-threshold strata the marginal CRC bound (P02 PM1). The tested cells pool strata over range and include fallback strata, for which the bound is only approximate or absent (P02 v03, pooled cells), and the test holds the fitted thresholds fixed, so it tests the risk of the fitted rule. The test adds: no evaluable group × axis × motion cell shown above δ, with power 0.8 against a true per-cell exceedance of 0.15 in each power-qualified cell; the pooled upper bound within δ + 0.03; the operability price on new data; the ranking against every comparator not shown above δ; and the premise's replication (H8).
- **What is reported beside it** (descriptive, never gated; `c1_analyse_v10`). For M\*, per tracker and population: the simultaneous one-sided 95 % upper bound of every evaluable cell, and their maximum, read as "M\*'s exceedance is at most U in every evaluable cell, simultaneously at 95 %". These bounds are **approximate**: their development coverage near δ is 0.78 at 39 units and 0.87 at 150 (Choice 50f).
- **No equivalence claim is added**, and family S keeps m = 5. Following Schuirmann's critique of reading a non-rejection as evidence, the paper reports U_max and HK1c's bound instead (section 6.8).
- **The paper's sentence** (Choice 52f) is in section 6.6.

**What a design choice may use (D16 Rule 0).** The literature; nuScenes development results; AV2 detector AP, tracker metrics and counts. No AV2 outcome. Section 10 lists what of this was seen on the confirmation logs. Every pass/fail criterion has its basis, class (PROVEN, STANDARD, PRACTICE, DEV or OWN) and confidence in register v05.

**One-shot commitment.** The 375-log AV2 set is used once for the claim family "validity and operability of DP-SCRC in the planner". A NO-GO leads to diagnosis and new work on new data, never to a re-run (section 9). Every run of a guarded C1 entry point on AV2 is recorded in the chained, append-only run log, which is uploaded with the results (section 8.6). Re-running the frozen analysis on the frozen inputs reproduces every table; this shows that the reported tables are the frozen analysis's, not that no other analysis was run (section 11.3).

**Outcome-blind attestation for this draft** (corrected in v07i: Choice 56d item 6; R20 re-check v03, m8). **No AV2 outcome has been computed or seen**, by the author or, to the author's knowledge, by anyone. The author of this plan ran no code on AV2 data for v05 to v07m and opened no AV2 file; the port's outcome-free builders (steps 1 to 8 of section 8.5) did run on AV2 inputs, on 5 Nov, and computed no outcome. For v07m the author read D16 v41 Choice 60 and R20's re-check v06, hashed the 48 added nuScenes training files against the frozen M plan v05's hash list, and reran the token check v02 and the block and slot checks. For v07l the author read D16 v40 Choice 59, R20's re-check v05, value check v02 and the input list of `c1_mstage_v06`, hashed the 28 nuScenes training files against the frozen M plan v05's hash list, and reran the token check v02 and the block and slot checks. For v07k the author read D16 v39 Choice 58 and R20's re-check v04; the argparse options of every tool of section 8.5 from step 16 on, the rehearsal driver `c1_rehearsal_v09` (l.150–240) and the value check; the frozen M plan v05's list of nuScenes training inputs and the RETUNED arms recorded in the nuScenes transfer fits; and the names (only) of the AV2 port's export, horizon and stage-A folders; and reran the token check v02 and the block and slot checks. For v07j the author read D16 v38 Choice 57, the shape check v02 and the descriptive-table code of `c1_analyse_v10` and its modules, and reran the token check v02 and the block and slot checks (section 11.1), which read file names, hashes and this text only. For v07i the author read D16 v37 Choices 55 and 56, R17g's check of code v10, R20's re-check v03, register v05, the rehearsal's configuration `70_TESTS/c1_v01/rehearsal150_v10/config.json` (nuScenes stand-in; its keys and shapes), the source of `c1_analyse_v10` and the modules it imports, `c1_rules_v02`, `c1_freeze_v07` and `c1_e0_v05`, the token check v02 and the shape check, and the headers of the two AV2 detector notes; and ran the token check v02 and the block and slot checks (section 11.1), which read file names, hashes and this text only. For v07h the author read D16 v35 Choice 54 and register v04, and reran the token, block and slot checks (section 11.1, row 156). For v07g the author read decision, review, code and results-note text only (D16 v34, R20's re-check v02, README v10, development check v03, the port's note v03, and the source of `c1_freeze_v07`), the GT inventory v02's paths, sizes, classes and hashes, and the rehearsal's status record; and ran the token check (section 11.4) and the block and slot checks (section 11.1, row 150), which read file names, hashes and this text only. The AV2 facts used are those already in v01 to v04, the own detector's note, the port's counts (outcome-free), and file-level facts (paths, sizes, dates and hashes) from R17e, R17f, R20, the lead and the inventory.

**Stand-ins are not evidence.** Every rehearsal of the confirmatory path (`70_TESTS/c1_v01/rehearsal150_v05b/` to `rehearsal150_v10/`) uses nuScenes validation scenes as "AV2" units and T1's data as AB3DMOT's. The AV2 paths (quarantine, inventory, provenance, data roles, run log, deposit, stage B, scene map) are tested in `test_c1_v13`'s copy tree on R17d's AV2-tagged stand-in. Their verdicts exercise the code only; none is quoted as a result.

## 1. Data and sets

### 1.1 The confirmation set (D18 v02 and v03, option A)

- **Split.** The 700 AV2 Sensor train log ids, sorted, permuted with NumPy `default_rng(20261102)`; the first 225 are held out, the other 475 train the detector. Split file `50_CODE/owndet/AV2_TRAIN_SPLIT_v01.json` (3771664f…), city composition in `50_CODE/owndet/AV2_TRAIN_SPLIT_v01_CITIES.json`; bound at the freeze as role `split`.
- **Detector.** Our own CenterPoint from LT3D's code (commit e33189aa…) with the public checkpoint's configuration (`centerpoint_0075voxel_second_secfpn_dcn_4x8_cyclic_50m_wide_hierarchy_tta_20e_av2`), trained from scratch on the 475 logs at every 5th sweep, batch 4, learning rate 5e-5, CBGS sampler, cyclic schedule over the iterations that fit the cap (`50_CODE/owndet/OWN_DETECTOR_DESIGN_ADDENDUM_A_v01.md` and `50_CODE/owndet/OWN_DETECTOR_DESIGN_ADDENDUM_B_v01.md`: 2 CBGS epochs if the projected total stays within $34, otherwise the largest count of at least 50,500 iterations). **No checkpoint or setting is selected on any of the 375 confirmation logs.** As run: section 1.2.
- **Confirmation set.** The own detector on the 225 held-out train logs plus the 150 validation logs, every 5th sweep: **375 logs, all unseen by the detector**. T1 (CANMOT `paper/local/canmot/sc`) and T3 (Poly-MOT `paper/baseline/poly_mot`) run on its detections with their nuScenes parameters, re-tuned on nothing. **AB3DMOT** (LT3D's, tracker label `tab`) runs on the same 2 Hz detections with LT3D's defaults, as a descriptive third tracker (Choice 38c; section 2).
- **The log list.** `av2_c1_logs_v01` writes `30_DATA/av2_trk_v02/C1_LOGS_375_v01.json` (the 225 held-out and the 150 validation ids, checked disjoint, 375 in all) and its `.sha256`; it is bound as `data:logs375`.
- **The AV2 port** (Choices 46a, 47a; README v10). The v01 AV2 builders read only `30_DATA/av2_val`, so they cannot read the 225 held-out train logs, and the laptop has too little disk for their lidar. README v10 binds as builders every port tool that writes a stage-A input, the log list, the frame check or the GT list (environments as in section 8.5):
  - `av2_common_v02` (a log-root resolver over `av2_val` and `av2_train225`; a sweep list from the recorded bucket listing when there is no local lidar) and `av2_fetch_v05` (no lidar contents);
  - `av2_c1_logs_v01` (the log list), `av2_c1_frame_check_v01` (the frame-time check) and `av2_c1_gt_list_v01` (the port's GT list, `30_DATA/av2_trk_v02/GT_FILES_FOR_QUARANTINE_v01.txt`);
  - `av2_gt_prep_v02`, `av2_ego_prep_v02` and `av2_tracker_input_v02`;
  - `av2_track_v02` (T1, T3), `av2_ab3dmot_v05` (AB3DMOT) and `av2_horizon_prep_v02` (the 2 s propagation);
  - and C1's own builders `c1_av2_scene_map_v01`, `c1_av2_gt_inventory_v01`, `c1_av2_provenance_v01` and `c1_power_counts_v05`.

  Its equivalence test `test_av2_port_v04_equiv_v01.py` is bound as a test module, and its run scripts as sources. The v01-era builders stay bound, because the 150-log public LT3D and VoxelNeXt manifests (H1-P, H5) were built with them. The Rule-0 tools `av2_track_metrics_v04` and `av2_hota_v04` are unbound, outcome-free metric tools. Stage A stays `av2_cache_stageA_v02`, which reads paths only and does not import `av2_common` (Choice 46e).
- **The port's acceptance rule** (Choice 46a, fixed before any 375-log output). On the 150 validation logs with the public LT3D detections, each new builder reproduces its v01 output byte for byte (port design v04 section 10). The listing-derived frame times of the 225 logs equal the own detector's frames (`av2_c1_frame_check_v01`). The equivalence records (`60_RESULTS/av2_trk/c1_375/equiv/`) are bound as `data:port_equiv`.
- **Disclosed** (Choices 49i, 52g item 9; R20 N10, re-check v02 m12). `av2_track_v02` and `av2_ab3dmot_v05` were corrected in place on 5 Nov at 07:07 EST, after their first resume test reused no checkpoint (Windows newline translation changed the checkpoint key; records kept in `equiv/superseded_checkpoint_hash_bug/`), before Choice 46's acceptance rule and before any 375-log output was **kept**: a production T1 worker had started on the pre-fix file at 07:06:01; it was stopped on purpose, its partial checkpoints are invalid under the new key, and it reran from 07:30 and passed (port note v03 section 8). The bound hashes are the corrected files'. The unbound metric tool `av2_hota_v04` agrees with v03 to a relative 1e-12, not bit for bit (v03 itself is not bit-reproducible; register C16c). Port tools are now versioned, never corrected in place.
- **A port failure** (Choice 47i; R20 P7). If a port builder fails its acceptance test, it is replaced only by a new versioned builder that passes the same test on the 150 validation logs; no 375-log output of the failed builder is read. If the 225 held-out logs cannot be built, the researcher decides, and this plan is redone; there is no silent fallback to 150 logs.
- **One prepared ground-truth file** (Choices 44f m5, 46b). `av2_gt_prep_v02 --logs-file 30_DATA/av2_trk_v02/C1_LOGS_375_v01.json` wrote one prepared ground-truth file covering exactly the 375 fold units, `30_DATA/av2_trk_v02/gt/av2_c1_gt375_v01.json` (e5c390aa…, 5 Nov), **before** stage A. Every 375-log stage-A manifest names it with its hash, and E⁰, the quarantine, stage B, the method stage and H8 use it.
- **Training data are not confirmation data.** The 475 training logs' annotations train the detector only.

### 1.2 Detector AP (reported, never gated; S20b, filled in v07c)

From `60_RESULTS/av2_det/AV2_OWN_DETECTOR_NOTE_v01.md` (4f014ae1…): the official av2 0.2.1 evaluator, 50 m, ROI-filtered, all 26 competition categories, ground truth restricted to the evaluated frames, every 5th sweep. The public checkpoint's row is from `60_RESULTS/av2_det/AV2_DETECTOR_NOTE_v01.md` (4c9a16d7…), the note of the public checkpoints; `60_RESULTS/av2_det/AV2_DETECTOR_NOTE_v02.md` (4481c774…) repeats that row beside the own detector's and leaves the rest of v01 standing.

| AP at 50 m | Vehicle | Pedestrian | Bicyclist | 26-class average |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Own detector, 150 validation logs (4,806 frames) | 0.857 | 0.777 | 0.403 | 0.425 |
| Own detector, 225 held-out logs (7,232 frames) | 0.854 | 0.800 | 0.495 | 0.415 |
| Public checkpoint, 150 validation logs | 0.875 | 0.795 | 0.543 | 0.469 |

"Vehicle" is the regular-vehicle class.

- **Training as run.** The final iteration count is **57,838**: 2 CBGS epochs ran, because the speed check projected $31.44, within the $34 cap (addendum B). The run was two segments on two hosts, joined by an exact resume at iteration 1,000 (optimiser, learning rate and schedule restored from the checkpoint); the data order after the resume is not the order an unbroken run would have drawn. The final checkpoint is `iter_57838.pth` (68369a71…). Every frame has predictions (4,806 of 4,806 validation frames, 7,232 of 7,232 held-out frames), with no non-finite values.
- **The bicyclist gap.** On the 150 validation logs the own detector's bicyclist AP is 0.403, against the public checkpoint's 0.543, a gap of 0.140; its 26-class average is 0.425 against 0.469. Vehicle and pedestrian are within 0.02. The own detector had a much shorter schedule (2 epochs against 20) on about two thirds of the data (475 logs against 700). The validation ground truth is byte-identical for the two detectors, so both validation rows use the same frames and labels. Other disclosures from the note: the sparse-convolution kernels printed a GPU-architecture warning on every call, without any error; training used every 5th sweep, against probably every 10th for the public checkpoint.
- **Nothing was selected on the confirmation logs** (R20 m9). D18 v02 (2 Nov 2026, 10:25 EST) fixed the assignment, the own detector on the 225 held-out and the 150 validation logs, before any AP of the own detector existed. There was no evaluation during training; AP was computed on the final checkpoint only, after training ended; the progress copy `iter_51000.pth` was never evaluated; and no checkpoint, setting or threshold was chosen on any of the 375 logs. The sentence in `60_RESULTS/av2_det/AV2_DETECTOR_NOTE_v02.md` that the choice of detector "is the lead's" was written after AP and decided nothing. AP is reported, never gated: the study tests calibration given a deployed detector (D18 v03), so a weaker two-wheeler detector is a condition of the test, not a reason to change it. Its effect on the two-wheeler cells can show only through the S20a counts and the floors, which are fixed by rule.
- **Seen before the lock** (Choice 49e). The detector AP was computed and seen before the text lock; it is disclosed here and changed no rule. Its detection-evaluation ground-truth feathers are in the GT inventory (section 8.4).

### 1.3 Descriptive data on the 150 validation logs

- **H1-P** (route `public_lt3d_val`): the public LT3D checkpoint (299cf3b7…) on the 150 validation logs, folds role `folds_val150`. Descriptive: its SD1_7 floor fails at 150 logs (power-qualified family 4, counts v02).
- **H5** (route `voxelnext_val`): the public VoxelNeXt checkpoint (8641d838…) on the same logs, folds role `folds_val150`. Descriptive: its checkpoint saw all 700 train logs, so it can only run on validation, where the floor fails.
- **Whether H1-P and H5 can run** (R20 open choice 13; Choice 47m). In the final rehearsal on README v10 (`70_TESTS/c1_v01/rehearsal150_v10/`; `70_TESTS/c1_v01/C1_V10_TEST_STATUS.json`, all pass), the route `public_lt3d_val` ran on both trackers and H1-P is reported "descriptive". The rehearsal's stand-in has no second detector, so the route `voxelnext_val` was not run there, and the decision reports H5 "not tested" with that reason, as Choice 38d requires. H5 uses the same entry points and folds role as H1-P. **On AV2 its route is required** (Choice 56d item 4; R20 re-check v03, m6): steps 22 to 24 of section 8.5 build its stage B, items, fits and runs, as for every route the configuration lists. A missing one is a deviation, recorded in the deviation log (section 9) and listed with the results (section 10, item 5), and H5 is then "not tested" with the reason. **R17g checked H5's path** (R17g section 9): in the analysis `voxelnext_val` takes exactly the branch that `public_lt3d_val` took in the rehearsal; the provenance tool's export and horizon checks pass on the two real VoxelNeXt 150-log stage-A manifests (its ground-truth and ego re-makes, which read annotations, were not run); stage B to the analysis on VoxelNeXt data first runs after the deposit. Both are descriptive in any case.
- **Q1 (QCNet) is "not tested" in C1** (Choice 44d, the fallback of Choice 41c; decided before any outcome, and replacing Choice 43(a)'s route). The reasons (R17e section 4):
  - **No C1 tracker index shares QCNet's history source.** QCNet's deployed histories come from a 10 Hz matched AB3DMOT run with T1's SF thresholds (`30_DATA/qcnet_av2_v03/`); C1's AB3DMOT is the 2 Hz run of Choice 11, and T1 and T3 are other trackers.
  - **The frozen coverage condition cannot hold.** In R17e's definition (the share of T1's, or T3's, LT3D 2 Hz audit records that QCNet predicted; QCNet note v03 section 3), QCNet predicted 95.7 % of T1's records (88,182 of 92,166) and 88,170 of T3's 92,124, so the route's `not_predicted == 0` fails under either tracker. QCNet note v01 section 6's 97.4 % (89,778 "in both") uses another definition (R20 m15).
  - **What follows.** The frozen config has no `q1` entry (Choice 43d as amended by 44d). No QCNet outcome is computed for the C1 report; if one is ever computed, it is exploratory and labelled so. Q1 never changed GO, H9 or family S. The QCNet notes v01 to v03 stay as records.
  - **In the code** (Choices 45c, 48b m-5). `c1_analyse_v10` refuses a config with a `q1` entry, prints Q1 as "not tested" with these two reasons in its own block, and never lists Q1 among the missing inputs; `c1_predarm_v07` refuses every QCNet option. `entry:c1_qcnet_summary` is no longer a role, and `c1_qcnet_summary_v01` is superseded and unbound.

### 1.4 Classes, groups, items, populations

- **Four groups** (Choice 8): car := `REGULAR_VEHICLE`; pedestrian := `PEDESTRIAN`; two-wheeler := `BICYCLIST` (ridden-two-wheeler rule RB-1) and `MOTORCYCLIST`; large := bus, truck and trailer categories (port design section 4.2).
- **K8 items** (H1 to H4, H9, H10): enforced (track, check time) pairs at decision states, considered tracks within 60 m and not behind, every separating axis at the reference pose (the logged pose at t_k + h, the decision mode's leash), split by the in-path flag (`k8_items_v08 --av2-stageB`).
- **Method-stage items** (H6a, H6b, H7, H8 only): M-PLAN section 3 (in-path, persistently paired, uncensored at k + h, separating axis, toward the ego), h = 2 s. H9 does **not** use them (section 6.4).
- **Strata**: group × range (from the sensor's CTRV-predicted ego) × axis × motion state; L1 to L3; n_min = 19 units (Angelopoulos et al. 2024, Thm 1: a finite threshold needs 1/(n + 1) ≤ δ; register C4).
- **Populations** (D14 section 4): PD7, SD1_7 (gap ≤ 5 m), PD (in-path), NIP (reported).
- **Cells**: group × axis × motion pooled over range (32) plus the pooled cell; **evaluable** at ≥ 39 logs (on AV2 a log is a scene; basis in section 6.1).
- **Scene-to-log map** (Choices 41d, 44f m4). The bound builder `c1_av2_scene_map_v01` writes `[{"name": u, "log_token": log}]` for every unit of the given stage-A manifests, `log` being the manifest's own log. On AV2, `av2_cache_stageA_v02` records log = scenario id, so the map is the identity, `[{"name": u, "log_token": u}]`. The builder refuses an existing output, a nuScenes manifest and two logs for one unit. The map is bound at the freeze as `data:scene_json` (an AV2 freeze requires it), and the freeze checks that it gives every fold unit its stage-A log. For every unit, `c1_mstage_v06` and `c1_h8_v06` check that its log in the map equals the stage-A manifests' `scenes[u]["log"]`, so the check confirms the unit set and names.

### 1.5 The outcome-blind boundary, and its scope (Choices 13, 34, 42, 44, 47d, 48, 49)

- **Allowed before the freeze:** the tracker's propagation of its mean and covariance; the ego's logged poses; the toward-ego normal; predicted-range strata; planner decision construction; K8 decision-mode item counts; existence of the ground-truth `track_uuid` at the target sweep; E⁰ from stage-A fields (Choice 31); the detector AP (seen before the text lock) and the tracker metrics (HOTA, AMOTA, MOTA, IDS; computed after the text lock), which use ground truth at the same sweep (Rule 0). The last three are computed before the quarantine.
- **Forbidden until the freeze and the external registration:** any ground-truth position, velocity or box at a sweep later than k; any residual, projected error, U, exceedance, margin, infeasibility, V2, TTV, NEES/ANEES or coverage.
- **Two stages.** Stage-A caches (`av2_cache_stageA_v02`) carry no annotation position, box, size or yaw at any frame (tracker note v02, "Not computed"; identity and existence only); they serve the counts, E⁰ and the freeze, and their manifests record the hash of the one prepared ground-truth file. The C1 steps that read ground truth after k are stage B (`av2_cache_stageB_v06`, entry `stageB`, which joins it to the tracker outputs), the method stage, H8 and the analysis's E⁰ recompute. All of them run only after the freeze **and** the external deposit, and read the ground truth only from a quarantine member, verifying its decompressed SHA-256. Stage B writes only `30_DATA/av2_cache_stageB_<tag>/` and logs each build to `40_PROTOCOL/C1_STAGE_B_LOG.txt` (section 8.6).
- **What the C1 entry points refuse** (the guarded entry points use `c1_guard_v05`, which imports `c1_guard_v04` and `c1_guard_v03`; `c1_freeze_v07` and `c1_quarantine_v03` use `c1_guard_v06`, which imports v05 and adds only their helpers; the scope of this statement is section 11.3):
  - one hard-coded hash log, `40_PROTOCOL/C1_PLAN_HASH_LOG.txt`, of which only the latest line is accepted, with no command-line override on AV2;
  - **every C1 entry point that reads ground truth after k, or a table built from it, requires the validated deposit record** (Choice 42a): stage B, items, fits, runs, method stage, H8, predictor arm and analysis. Only `entry:c1_e0`, `entry:c1_folds` and `entry:c1_freeze` are exempt. The folds and the freeze read no ground truth; `c1_e0_v05` uses only outcome-free fields (existence and identity) of a file that also holds later positions (R20 m7). Every runtime record and every table carries `deposit_record_sha256`, which must equal the current record;
  - a dataset tag must agree with the unit names;
  - run-time hash checks of the entry point and every loaded project module; the running Python, NumPy and SciPy versions must equal the plan's FROZEN_ENV block; the Trajectron++ keys wrapper's versions must equal FROZEN_ENV and the run wrapper's FROZEN_ENV_TPP (Choice 44e), checked first on a dry record by `c1_tpp_envcheck_v01` before any key or run is made (Choice 45a);
  - on AV2 an existing output is never overwritten, and every guarded run writes a "start" and a "done" line to the chained, append-only run log (section 8.6).
- **Quarantine before the freeze** (Choices 42b, 44a, 47b, 48a, 49b, 49d). Every ground-truth file of the GT inventory (every workspace file that holds per-object AV2 annotation geometry, by the content rule of section 8.4), and **each AV2 stage-A manifest's ground-truth file**, is moved into a verified gzip quarantine before the freeze. The freeze refuses while any recorded ground-truth hash has a plain copy in the workspace outside the skipped folders, while the quarantine folder holds anything but recorded members and its manifest, or while a recorded stage-A member's manifest is missing from its recorded path (section 8.4).
- **Superseded entry points cannot compute AV2 outcomes** (Choices 36, 39a). Byte-exact originals are archived as gzip members in `superseded_originals/`; in each superseded file only `main()` was edited, to refuse any input whose dataset, read from outcome-free tags and names, is not nuScenes or synthetic. Older entry points are refused for want of a plan hash log before the freeze, and by role after it (section 8.8).
- **Scope** (Choice 47d; R20 P1). These are properties of the C1 entry points. Not technically barred were: the programs of the earlier nuScenes studies; the public AV2 annotation files of all 375 logs, which are on the analysis computer (`30_DATA/av2_val/` and `30_DATA/av2_train225/`); the AV2 data tools, bound and unbound, which can rebuild a prepared ground-truth file from those annotations; and copies outside the workspace. The embargoed OSF registration is made after the freeze and before any C1 step that reads ground truth after k. The claim is stated verbatim in section 11.3, and the words "outcome-blind boundary enforced by code" are not used without that scope.

### 1.6 Counts

`[SLOT: S20a stage-A counts of the 375 logs, T1 and T3, own detector]`

To be reported: decisions, items per population, per cell and per log; evaluable cells; power-qualified families and their record coverage; method-stage in-path cells; H9's PD items per cell; AB3DMOT's counts; per-fold source and city composition. H9's history-length counts need the Trajectron++ keys, which are built from the stage-B cache; they are reported by the frozen predictor arm at analysis (section 6.4). The counts of the 150 validation logs under the public detectors (counts v01, v02) stay as reported in v02 to v04; they are not the H1 layout.

## 2. Claims under test

δ = 0.05 throughout: a calibration level for each relied constraint, not a safety target (register C1). Formulas and levels in sections 6.2 and 6.3. "Secondary" claims are pre-registered; "descriptive" ones are reported in full and never claimed.

| ID | Label | Data | Statement |
|---|---|---|---|
| **H1** | **Primary** | 375 logs, own detector, T1 and T3 | **Form B**: no cell, family cell or pooled cell of M\* is shown above δ on PD7, SD1_7 or PD, the floors and HK1c hold, and M\* is less often infeasible than every comparator not shown above δ (Choices 9, 47e, 50b, 52c). Expected to turn on B6 on T3 (E12) |
| H2 | Secondary | as H1 | **Form C**: the decision-mode price, M\* − B4 infeasible rate, is ≤ c_t on T1 and T3; c_T1 = c_T3 = 0.03 (development prices −0.084 and −0.047; E11; register C10a) |
| H3 | Secondary | as H1 | **M\*Jx**, the trajectory-level certificate (Choice 21): its joint loss is not shown above δ on PD7, SD1_7 and PD, and Form B holds on the joint loss, with the stricter comparator rule and a doubled p-value (Choices 49c, 52c) |
| H4 | Secondary | as H1, transfer fits | **Transfer in the loop**: H1's formula with every fitted arm from the all-850-scene nuScenes fit (Choices 6, 7), with H4's own removal level and a doubled p-value (Choice 53b) |
| H6a | Secondary | 375 logs | **Method stage within AV2 (E1-AV2)**: M\* shown above δ in no E1-AV2 cell, fixed-rule and refit-CV, T1 and T3 |
| H6b | Secondary | 375 logs | **Practitioner failure**: B4 fails (flagged, M\* not flagged, paired difference positive; refit-CV) in at least one E1-AV2 cell on T1 and on T3, decided by its p-value (Choice 52e) |
| H7 | Secondary | 375 logs | **Method stage, transfer (E2-AV2)**: the M-stage M\* fitted on the 700 nuScenes train scenes is shown above δ in no AV2 cell and not in the pooled cell, T1 and T3 |
| H8 | Secondary (family P) | exactly the units of the `folds` role (the 375 logs), T1 | **Diagnosis replicates**: an in-path car lateral 2 s cell (< 20 m or 20 to 40 m) FAILS under B0, and its truth-oriented exceedance also FAILS |
| H9 | **Descriptive** (S20c; Choice 52b) | 375 logs, own-detector T1 and T3 histories, K8 relied in-path items (PD) | **Trajectron++ `int_ee`**: DP-SCRC with history-length strata, cross-fitted on the C1 folds, along the K8 tracker-planner's constraints; its estimates, cell bounds, power-qualified E2 family and floor status are reported as descriptive results (section 6.4). **No confirmatory claim is made about a learned predictor** |
| H10 | Secondary | as H1 | **Motion-strata ablation, M\* against B6**: B6 fails (flagged, paired difference positive) in at least one PD7 cell where M\* is not flagged, and M\*'s infeasible rate is at most 0.03 above B6's |

**What GO establishes** (Choice 47g; R20 P3). GO establishes, per tracker on 375 AV2 logs: M\* is not shown above δ in any evaluable cell, family or pooled cell of PD7, SD1_7 and PD; the floors hold; and M\* is less often infeasible than every comparator not shown above δ. B3's margin is at least M\*'s on every item (P02 PM3), so its infeasible rate cannot fall below M\*'s wherever infeasibility is monotone in the margin. A comparison with B3, and with RETUNED where it equals B0, is reported but is not evidence of operability. On T3 the development data predict that GO requires B6 to be shown above δ (E12; R16 M2). The decision states, for each comparator, why it is in or out of V_t. Power ≥ 0.80 is claimed only for the power-qualified cells (register C7b; section 6.6). Validity is claimed against per-cell excesses of about 0.15 and above, not smaller ones (sections 6.9, 7).

**Descriptive.** Form A; H1-P and H5 (section 1.3); **H9** (section 6.4); M\*'s simultaneous one-sided 95 % cell upper bounds and their maximum, labelled approximate (section 0); the criteria audit's outputs of Choice 50(f) (section 7); the source heterogeneity check and the train-to-validation source-transfer reading (Choice 22), which change no claim whatever they show (R20 open choice 18); **AB3DMOT (`tab`)** as a third tracker on the 375 logs with the same items, folds and entry points as T1 and T3, reported in the validity and trade-off tables, outside H1 and outside family S (Choice 38c); its descriptive method stage is not computed (Choice 58b; section 6.1); **H9's seed robustness** (S1-AV2, Choice 38b): the same H9 analysis with forward-pass seeds 20261104 and 20261105, labelled "seed-robust" only if H9's descriptive pattern is the same under all three seeds (Choice 54c; the label decides nothing); loop mode (section 6.6); V2 and its variants for every arm (section 6.6); RETUNED and RETUNED-HK1 (section 6.6); M\*J (Choice 21); H9-noHL (Choice 37); H9-T, the nuScenes-fitted Trajectron++ transfer (Choices 19, 23); TPPgt, Trajectron++ with ground-truth histories (upper bound); A6 calibration diagnostics; A7 sharpness; the α-sweep and δ′ arms; A13; A14; NIP; margin ratios; HOTA, AMOTA, MOTA and IDS; the AV2 pairing rate by score bin (register C15h); how often v_max binds (C15f); the share of relied constraints at 50 to 60 m (C15d); the decision forms of family S (section 6.3).

**Not tested in C1:** Q1 (QCNet), for the two reasons in section 1.3 (Choice 44d).

**Not run in this plan** (outside the bound path of README v10): the 10 Hz protocol; the Ford Fusion footprint sensitivity; T2 on the 375 logs; any QCNet outcome; D17's sensitivities S2 (seed-pooled V2, Choice 27) and the trace > 100 m² sensitivity (Choice 28) (R20 m11). C1's H9 uses `int_ee`, whatever map data arrive; `int_ee_me` is not used in C1 (Choices 3, 30; R20 m12).

**Not claimed.** A closed-loop guarantee; a safety ranking from loop-mode collisions; validity in non-evaluable cells; power in cells outside the power families; validity against per-cell excesses smaller than about 0.15; validity along an object's true axes; an equivalence bound on any cell; any confirmatory statement about a learned predictor (H9 is descriptive); anything outside the four groups; anything about the AV2 test split; that no early look at the AV2 annotations was technically possible outside the C1 entry points (section 11.3).

## 3. Calibration: what is calibrated where

### 3.1 Folds (Choice 22)

- **375 logs: 5 folds of 75** (role `folds`), from `c1_folds_v03 --dataset av2 --split … --val …` (entry `c1_folds`): the 375 log ids sorted, permuted by NumPy `default_rng(20261112)`, cut into 5 consecutive blocks (`np.array_split`). The tool checks that the ids are exactly the split's 225 held-out logs plus the 150 validation logs, and records the NumPy version and a permutation probe. Not stratified by source; source is reported.
- **Why.** Split CRC is applied per fold, so PM1 applies per fold (Angelopoulos et al. 2024; register C14). A uniformly random partition keeps every test log exchangeable with every calibration log, which is the certificate's assumption (P02 PM1; S02 v03 D4, D5; the transductive guarantee of P01 Thm 5 on the pooled set). Stratifying by source would add an assumption for little balance: the validation share per fold is 0.40 ± 0.05, the ± being the hypergeometric standard deviation (0.051; R20 m10). Source as a stratum would split the logs again and bring back the floor problem.
- **Two child fold files, no new randomness** (Choice 38d). `c1_folds_v03 --restrict-from <375-log folds> --source val` writes role `folds_val150`, and `--source held_out_train` writes role `folds_train225`. Each keeps its parent's fold labels restricted to that source and records `parent_sha256`. The freeze and the guard's `check_child_folds` check that each child is exactly the parent's units of its source, with the parent's labels.
- **Which folds each route uses** (`c1_analyse_v10`):

| Route | Folds role | Claims |
|---|---|---|
| `within_lt3d` (own detector) | `folds` | H1, H2, H3, H10 (and H6, H9 on the same folds) |
| H8 (`c1_h8_v06`) | exactly the `folds` units (config `h8_units` = `folds`; Choice 42d) | H8 |
| `transfer_lt3d` | `folds` | H4 |
| `public_lt3d_val` | `folds_val150` | H1-P, descriptive |
| `voxelnext_val` | `folds_val150` | H5, descriptive |
| `source_transfer` | fit on `folds_train225`'s units (a `source` fit by `entry:c1_fit`), applied to `folds_val150`'s units, which must be disjoint | descriptive |
| AB3DMOT (`tab`) | `folds` | descriptive |
| Q1 (QCNet) | none: not tested (section 1.3) | none |

- **Source reported** (descriptive, pre-specified; it changes no claim): per-fold source and city composition; M\*'s pooled V1 on PD7, SD1_7 and PD and its infeasible rate by source, with a log-bootstrap interval for the difference; the source-transfer reading above (route index 4).

### 3.2 Routes

| Arm | Within AV2 (primary; H1, H2, H3, H6, H8 to H10) | Transfer (H4, H7) |
|---|---|---|
| K8 M\* (`Mstar_ip`), B1, B3, B4, B6, B9 | each log's thresholds from the fit on the other 4 folds (`c1_fit_v06 --route within`, entry `c1_fit`), exact CRC | the all-850-scene nuScenes fit, `60_RESULTS/c1_transfer_fits_v01/c1_transfer_fits_{t1,t3}_v01.json` (97f4b78c…, ac4cbda2…), applied by `c1_transfer_apply_v01` |
| RETUNED | cross-fitted selection on the merged α/δ′ curve (21 points) over the AV2 folds (D14 A3's pooled rule; register A4) | the nuScenes point chosen by the same rule: B0a1 (T1), B0d0.3 (T3) (`retuned_transfer_{t1,t3}.txt`); required for H4 (Choice 25) |
| M\*Jx (H3) | for test fold f (0 to 4), λ is fitted on fold g = (f + 1) mod 5 and the base is refitted by M\*'s rule on the other three folds (225 logs); λ is applied to fold f; exact λ decision (`c1_fit_v02`'s rule, used by `c1_fit_v06`; R20 m8) | descriptive |
| M\*J | M\*'s base, λ on its 4 calibration folds; descriptive | descriptive |
| Method-stage M\* and baselines | E1-AV2: 20 repetitions of a 5-fold split of the 375 logs (seeds 20261113 + r), exact CRC in every fit and refit; the repetitions' MC SE is reported (register A6) | the 700-scene nuScenes train fits (M-PLAN E2), two-sample refit log bootstrap |
| Trajectron++ (H9) | DP-SCRC with history-length strata on the C1 folds (`folds`; section 6.4) | H9-T: the nuScenes val fit, descriptive |
| QCNet (Q1) | not run in C1 (Q1 not tested; section 1.3) | none |
| B0, Z0, α-sweep, δ′ arms, LOG | fit-free | fit-free |

**Two transfer sources** (R20 m14). H7 is the method stage's own transfer test (M-PLAN E2), whose frozen definition fits on the 700 nuScenes train scenes; H4 transfers K8's decision-mode arms, fitted on all 850 nuScenes scenes (Choice 7). The two claims keep their own frozen definitions.

**Exact arithmetic (E15).** Every CRC accept/reject decision (every fit, refit and λ) is decided in exact rational arithmetic (`c1_crc_exact_v01.crc_threshold_exact`).

**Ordering.** The transfer fits, the split and the folds exist and are hashed before the freeze; every within-AV2 fit is made after the freeze and the deposit, by frozen code, from stage-B items, and is checked by provenance (Choice 24).

**`c1_transfer_apply_v01` is unguarded by design** (Choice 43c; R17e section 5). It imports only the standard library, reads only the nuScenes transfer fit and the units of the folds file, copies the fit unchanged (asserted), and computes no statistic. Given ground truth as the fit it refuses, and given it as the folds it fails before writing anything. Its output is checked by hash downstream: `c1_run_v06` requires the source fit bound as `transfer_fits`, and the analysis checks that the applied fit equals its source.
