## 5. Modes and endpoints (D14 sections 2 and 4, unchanged)

- **Decision mode** carries H1 to H4 and H10: one decision per keyframe from the logged ego's state, identical states for every arm, leash to the log, v_max = log maximum + 2 m/s, 32 accelerate-then-brake candidates and the emergency stop, object-axis half-spaces with a 0.5 m buffer every 0.25 s to 2 s, exemption rule C8, back-off ladder λ ∈ {0.75, 0.5, 0.25, 0}, recorded tracks at score ≥ 0.4, deployable covariance with σ_a = 0.10 m. The bases of these settings are register rows C15a to C15h and C12c.
- **Planner sensitivities** (Choice 50g; register C15c to C15e). Descriptive only, and they block nothing: the buffer at 0.25 and 1.0 m, a denser candidate set, and the shares reported by range and horizon. They run on nuScenes development after the text lock (R20 re-check v02, m14), and they cannot change a frozen setting.
- **Loop mode**: non-reactive closed-loop replay, delays 0, 1, 2 s; descriptive (section 6.6).
- **Endpoints**: V1 per-constraint exceedance; O1 infeasible-decision rate (O2 progress and speed, O3 back-off, descriptive); S1 V2_viol, collisions, front-buffer intrusions; S2 TTV < 4 s and `ttv_enf`, with TTV < 2 s labelled "arm-invariant by construction"; the joint loss J_P; C1 to C4 calibration and sharpness; method-stage cell exceedance and margins.

---

## 6. Statistics, GO condition and multiplicity

### 6.1 Units, bootstrap, p-values, seeds, the power rule and the frozen configuration (Choices 47j, 47k, 50d, 50e, 52d, 53c, 53d)

- **Unit and estimand.** The log; the log-level mean of each log's rate (P01 Thm 5, P02 PM1; register A1). The log is the bootstrap unit (Davison and Hinkley 1997 section 3.8; Field and Welsh 2007; register C3a).
- **Bootstrap: B = 40000** (Choice 53c; was 2000, then 20000 by Choice 52d) for every bootstrap of the analysis: the flags, the pooled and paired bounds, HK1c and the p-values. Weights are shared by every arm, and `c1_analyse_v10` refuses any other B outside its smoke mode.
  - **Our rule** (class OWN; Andrews and Buchinsky 2000 choose B for a target Monte Carlo accuracy): every decision quantile has at least 10 replicates beyond it. The most extreme is H4's removal quantile, 0.0025/9 ≈ 0.00028, which needs B ≥ 36000. With 40000 the tail counts are about 11 (H4's removal), 33 (H3's removal), 56 (H1's removal), 200 (HK1c) and **at least 71 (Form B)**: K has 7 comparators, so |V_t| ≤ 7, and 40000 × 0.0125/7 ≈ 71 on H1's routes and 40000 × 0.025/14 ≈ 71 on H4's; 250 holds only for |V_t| ≤ 2, the development case. This corrects v07h's "at least 250" (Choice 56d item 1, an erratum to Choice 53c; R20 re-check v03, m3; register v05, C3b). Every count is above 10.
  - **Unchanged:** the power rule's 2000 datasets and its first 500 weight rows (a prefix independent of B); the descriptive arms' 500 power datasets; the predictor arm's B = 2000 (H9 is descriptive); and the method stage's own B = 2000 for the fixed rule and 200 refit-CV replicates (`c1_mstage_v06` is not reopened).
  - **Cost** (README v10, measured): about 9 more minutes per 375-log table than at B = 2000, and about 5 GB peak process memory; the analysis runs one route and tracker at a time. A `MemoryError` or a disk-full error, raised or recorded in a decision, is an environment stop, and the same frozen command reruns under the next output name (section 9).
  - **HK1c's L** was calibrated at B = 2000; at B = 20000 M\*'s pooled-UB coverage at L = 0.995 is unchanged within Monte Carlo error (check v03, part A3).
- **Monte Carlo error of the decisive bounds** (Choice 53e). On development the MC SE of every decisive bound was at most 0.0006 at B = 20000, and V_t and V^J membership were identical in 100 of 100 seed sets (check v03, part E3). On AV2, the independent results review (R21) computes, after the run, the MC SE of the decisive bounds (the removal statistics, Form B, HK1c, and the p-values of H3 and H4) from 20 independent seed sets and the Andrews–Buchinsky formula, and the seed-set stability of V_t and V^J membership. It is descriptive; the verdict is always the primary seed's.
- **Inference is conditional on the fitted thresholds** (Choice 47k; R20 P9). The log bootstrap holds each route's fitted thresholds fixed, so it tests the risk of the fitted rule; the method stage's refit-CV estimates the marginal quantity. The pre-registered seeds decide every status. R21 also reports, descriptively, the share of 20 alternative seed sets under which each H1 flag, floor and Form B bound holds, every cell with |t − q| < 0.2, and every change of a power-family size.
- **Every Monte Carlo p-value is (k + 1)/(B + 1)**, never k/B, k being the replicates on the null side (Choice 53d; Davison and Hinkley 1997 ch. 4; Phipson and Smyth 2010). It is never 0 and is conservative for the Monte Carlo step at any B; the bootstrap p-value itself is valid only approximately (register v05, C3b; R20 re-check v03, m2). k and B are recorded beside each p. The p-values of family S are:
  - **H2:** k = the replicates with Δ(B4) > c;
  - **H3:** min(1, 2·|V^J|·max_c p_c), the doubled p (Choice 52c);
  - **H4:** min(1, 2·|V_t|·max_c p_c) on the transfer route, the doubled p (Choice 53b);
  - **H10:** the larger of the cell part's sup-t p and the NI part's p;
  - **H6b:** the method stage's smallest per-cell p (per cell, the larger of B4's sup-t p and the paired B4 − M\* p, where M\* is not flagged), converted from its recorded k/refit_B to (k + 1)/(refit_B + 1). With refit_B = 200 the smallest possible p is 1/201 ≈ 0.004975, so **H6b can pass at Holm's smallest level, 0.005, only with k = 0**; the cost is power, not validity.

  Here p_c = (k_c + 1)/(B + 1), k_c the replicates with f_{M\*} − f_c ≥ 0. H1's own Form B p, |V_t| times the largest p_c, is reported against its threshold 0.0125, but H1 is decided by its bound (section 6.3).

| Use | Seed |
|---|---|
| Folds, 375 logs; the child files `folds_val150` and `folds_train225` are restrictions of it, with no new randomness | 20261112 |
| K8 log bootstrap, decision mode | [20261110, i, j], i = 0 T1, 1 T3, 2 AB3DMOT (descriptive) |
| K8 power rule (floors) | [20261111, i, j] |
| Loop mode | [20261110, i, j, 1] |
| Route index j | 0 within AV2 (H1, H2, H3, H10); 1 transfer (H4); 2 VoxelNeXt on validation (H5); 3 public LT3D on validation (H1-P); 4 source transfer; 5 predictor arm (H9, H9-noHL) |
| Method stage (H6, H7) | 20261113: E1 folds 20261113 + r (r = 0 to 19), refit-CV 20261113 + 1000 + b, bootstrap generators [20261113, crc32(label)] |
| H8 bootstrap | [20261113, 8], B = 2000 |
| E⁰ (H8) | 20261114 (asserted by `c1_e0_v05` and by the analysis's recompute) |
| Trajectron++ forward pass | 20261102 (primary; the H9 table must come from it); 20261104 and 20261105 (seed robustness, descriptive) |
| H9 bootstrap and power | [20261110, i, 5] and [20261111, i, 5], i the tracker index (0 T1, 1 T3) |
| Detector split | 20261102 |

- **Cell flag**: one-sided simultaneous sup-t over the arm's evaluable cells, at the level section 6.2 gives for its use; zero SE with Ê > δ flags. A comparator's non-finite quantile is recorded as q = None and its cells are judged by Ê > δ. **A family in which M\* has zero exceedance in every bootstrap draw** (a non-finite quantile) is judged the same way, by Ê > δ; it does not raise (`c1_analyse_v10`; R20 m13). Every zero-SE case, and its effect on V_t, is reported (Choice 50f; register A3).
- **The power rule** (Choices 50d, 50e; register C5 to C7; `c1_rules_v02`, the same code as `c1_rules_v01.power_target`). A cell is power-qualified if its simulated power to flag a true per-cell exceedance of **0.15** in its population's all-evaluable one-sided sup-t family at 0.95 (`POWER_FLAG_LEVEL`, for every arm, comparators included) is **≥ 0.80**. The power family is the set of power-qualified cells; the floors count them.
  - **0.80:** the conventional type II error of 10 to 20 % (ICH E9 section 3.5; Cohen 1988).
  - **0.15, kept and anchored:** the median exceedance of the comparator cells flagged on development (0.151 over 116 cells); ICH E9 section 3.5 names the anticipated effect as a basis for power. **Caveat:** flagged cells are selected for size; over all evaluable cells of B4 and B6 above δ on PD7 the medians are 0.11 to 0.14. P = 0.10 is the only value that flips a development verdict on the 840 scenes (M\*'s SD1_7 floor fails on T1, and on T3 with more datasets); at 375 units and P = 0.12 the SD1_7 floor fails in every development set (section 7).
  - **Target-only planting:** the tested cell is planted at 0.15 and every other evaluable cell at exactly δ, with the same beta-binomial ρ and the arm's real record counts, so no observed outcome enters the rule. This is individual power, the quantity the power sentence states (Senn and Bretz 2007). Development families under it: 27 / 18 / 5 (T1) and 26 / 18 / 5 (T3) on PD7 / SD1_7 / PD (26 / 18 / 5 on both in the development replay v02).
  - **Beta-binomial ρ = 0.5, kept and derived:** it is at or above the upper 80 % limit of every pooled development ANOVA ICC (Ridout, Demétrio and Firth 1999) on PD7 and SD1_7 for M\*, B4 and B6 (largest 0.341), following the upper-limit planning practice of Browne 1995 and Turner, Prevost and Thompson 2004. **Caveats:** the scene-unweighted moment estimate reaches 0.448 (B6, T1 SD1_7; M\*'s largest is 0.423), still below 0.5; per-cell ICCs at exceedances of 0.10 to 0.20 have medians 0.42 to 0.48 but a 90th percentile of 0.67 to 0.71, so 0.5 is not conservative for every single cell. Over ρ 0.2 to 0.7 at P ≥ 0.125, no development verdict changes.
  - **Datasets per arm** (Choice 52g item 4): **2000** simulated datasets for M\* and the 7 comparators of K (was 200; Morris, White and Crowther 2019: the MC SE at power 0.8 falls from 0.028 to 0.009, and a cell at true power 0.78 qualifies with probability ≈ 0.02 instead of ≈ 0.28; **caveat:** near the floor, at P 0.10, 200 datasets had decided by chance), and **500** for the 24 arms whose validity is descriptive only (the 21 sweep points, Z0, M\*J, M\*Jx and RETUNED-HK1), for cost (`N_DATASETS_DESCRIPTIVE_ARMS`).
  - **The first 500 bootstrap weights** (500 and 2000 give identical development families; register C6e), and **nested seeds [20261111, i, j]**; the datasets are drawn in batches of 25, so a run's datasets are a prefix of a longer run's.
  - **F_MIN = 6** power-qualified cells on PD7 and SD1_7: our own value; F_MIN 4 to 8 changes no development verdict, and at 375 development units and P = 0.15 the floor holds in every set (SD1_7 family median 11, 10th percentile 9 to 10). No source sets a minimum count of powered strata (register C7a).
  - **PD:** at least one evaluable cell and no flag in its own power-qualified family; our own rule, and PD validity is claimed for its power-qualified cells only (register C7b).
  - **Evaluable at ≥ 39 logs:** our own value, taken from P01 Prop 6(ii), a calibration-size result; no coverage or size criterion derives it. The development check shows the flag is conservative at every count from 10 to 150 units (size 0.004 to 0.026), so 39 does not inflate false flags; it is not a coverage claim for upper bounds (register C5).
- **Paired operability**: per-log differences at identical decision states; percentile log-bootstrap bounds (Efron and Tibshirani 1993; register C3c). Development coverage at 375 units is 0.968 to 0.983 at 0.975 and 0.982 to 0.993 at 0.9875, every contrast at or above Bradley's lower limit of 0.9625; three bounds are slightly below nominal (section 6.9).
- **R\*'s constants in code.** Every value of sections 6.1 and 6.2 is a named constant in `c1_rules_v02` (bound module), with a one-line source comment, pinned by `test_c1_v13` (group CRIT): `ALPHA_AGAINST` 0.05; `ALPHA_FOR` 0.025; `ALPHA_REMOVAL` and `ALPHA_FORM_B` 0.0125 each; `ALPHA_REMOVAL_VJ` 0.0025 with `H3_P_MULTIPLIER` 2; `ALPHA_REMOVAL_H4` 0.0025 with `H4_P_MULTIPLIER` 2 on `H4_ROUTE` "transfer_lt3d"; `MC_P_ADD` 1; `HK1C_LEVEL` 0.995, `HK1C_MARGIN` 0.03, `DELTA` 0.05; `P_PLANT` 0.15, `RHO` 0.5, `POWER_MIN` 0.80; `N_DATASETS` 2000, `N_DATASETS_DESCRIPTIVE_ARMS` 500, `N_WEIGHTS` 500, `POWER_FLAG_LEVEL` 0.95; `F_MIN` 6, `PD_MIN_EVALUABLE` 1, `MIN_UNITS` 39; `B_BOOT` 40000; `H9_LABEL_C1` "descriptive"; `C_FORM_C` and `NI_MARGIN` 0.03; `FORM_A_K` 4; `HOLM_M` 5. A config that states one of them with another value is refused by `c1_rules_v02.config_check`, which the lead runs on the real config at step 19a, before the freeze, and `c1_analyse_v10 --c1` runs again; the freeze does not run it (R20 re-check v03, M-2). The lead verified README v10, including these constants, before this draft.

**Frozen analysis configuration** (Choices 47j, 54c, 55b, 56b, 57c, 58b, 58e, 58f; R20 P8; R17g M-2; R20 re-check v03, M-2). The config file is bound as role `config`. **Its structure is exactly that of the rehearsal's working configuration**, `70_TESTS/c1_v01/rehearsal150_v10/config.json` (SHA-256 9e26213e2121facbb0b25a43d3a3654a85177c62f01da6845e13316c173f463a, pinned and uploaded with the deposit, Choice 58f; nuScenes stand-in; its key structure only): the same keys at every level, with the same shapes, and the AV2 files in place of the stand-in's. The one difference is the number of route objects: five on AV2, four in the rehearsal, which left out `voxelnext_val`. `<…>` marks a value; file references are to the files bound under the named roles, whose hashes the freeze writes into FROZEN_HASHES.

```
{
  "routes": [                                  a list of route objects, one per route (five on AV2):
    {
      "name":  "within_lt3d",                  one of the five route names of section 3.1, each once
      "runs":  <the folder holding run_record_<tag>_decide.json and episodes_<tag>_decide.jsonl>,
      "tags":  {"t1": <tag>, "t3": <tag>, "tab": <tag>},                  "tab" on within_lt3d only
      "fits":  {"t1": <fit file>, "t3": <fit file>, "tab": <fit file>},   one fit per key of "tags"
      "folds": <the file of the route's folds role, section 3.1>,
      "modes": ["decide"]
    },
    … the same six keys for transfer_lt3d, public_lt3d_val, voxelnext_val and source_transfer
  ],
  "units_are_logs": true,
  "manifests":      [<stage-A MANIFEST.json path>, …],      a flat list of paths, never a map
  "c":              {"t1": 0.03, "t3": 0.03},               required under --c1
  "folds":          <the file bound as role folds>,
  "folds_val150":   <the file bound as role folds_val150>,
  "folds_train225": <the file bound as role folds_train225>,
  "e0": {
    "plan_values":   {"car|<20|lat": <S21>, "car|20-40|lat": <S21>},
    "seed":          20261114,
    "sigma_a":       0.1,
    "inputs":        {"workers": [<export folder>, …], "gt": <path>, "meta": <folder>, "horizon": <path>},
    "inputs_sha256": {<path>: <SHA-256>, …}
  },
  "method_stage":   {"t1": <table>, "t3": <table>},
  "h8":             <table>,                                one path, never a per-tracker map
  "h8_units":       "folds",
  "h9":             {"t1": <table>, "t3": <table>},
  "h9_label":       "descriptive",
  "h9_descriptive": {"tab": <table>},
  "note":           <text>                                  optional
}
no other key: no "q1", no "method_stage_descriptive" (not computed), no "criteria" and no restated constant
```

- **The keys** (R17g section 8; R20 re-check v03 section 3).
  - `routes`: one object per route with exactly the six keys shown, as in the rehearsal. `runs` is the folder of the route's run records. `tags` and `fits` name T1 and T3, and `tab` on `within_lt3d` only; `fits` has one fit file per tag (required under `--c1`). `folds` and `modes` are carried as in the rehearsal; `c1_analyse_v10` does not read them, and takes each route's folds from its frozen role (section 3.1; `c1_analyse_v06.ROUTE_FOLDS_ROLE`). There is no `retuned_arm`, as in the rehearsal; the analysis then takes RETUNED's arm from the route's fit (`retuned_transfer_arm`).
  - `manifests`: the stage-A manifests bound as role `stageA_manifest` (section 8.1), as a flat list. The analysis reads the list before any decision and outside any exception handler, so a map would stop it before any decision (R17g M-2).
  - `e0`: `plan_values` is S21's `H8_E0_primary` for the two decision cells; `seed` is 20261114 (`E0.SEED`); `sigma_a` is 0.10 m; `inputs` holds the `--workers`, `--gt`, `--meta` and `--horizon` arguments of the E⁰ run (section 8.5, step 14); `inputs_sha256` is that run's recorded `inputs_sha256` (S21). A missing `seed` or `plan_values` would make the E⁰ check fail and H8 "not tested" after the outcomes exist.
  - `h8` is one path; a per-tracker map is refused, and H8 is then "not tested". `method_stage` and `h9` map T1 and T3, and `h9_descriptive` maps `tab`, each to its table of record (section 8.6).
  - **`method_stage_descriptive` is not in the AV2 config: AB3DMOT's descriptive method stage is not computed** (Choice 58b, an erratum to Choice 57b; R20 re-check v04, m1). The bound `c1_mstage_v06` asserts that its training exports are nuScenes, and it loads both its sets with the one `--experiment`, whose id `m_data_v04.load_split` requires of every export. AB3DMOT's exports carry the id `lt3d/ab3dmot` and exist only for AV2, so the bound code cannot make the table. The key stays optional in `c1_analyse_v10` (`DESC_TABLES`, from `c1_analyse_v06`) and in shape check v02. Had it been given, the decision's `descriptive_trackers` block would record only whether the table is valid (`ok`, `why`), never its numbers; v07j's statement that AB3DMOT's H6 and H7 rows would be reported from it is withdrawn.
  - **`folds_val150` and `folds_train225` are not read by the analysis.** The files they name are written by the folds step (step 12) and bound by the freeze through its options `--folds-val150` and `--folds-train225`; each route reads its folds from the frozen role. The keys are kept because the rehearsal's configuration has them and the shape check requires every key it has.
  - **Not in the config:** `q1` (refused by the freeze and the analysis); `method_stage_descriptive` (above); `criteria` and any restated constant (the code accepts them only with its own values, but the shape check refuses any key that is neither in the rehearsal's configuration nor one of its two optional keys); any other key.
- **Who checks it** (Choices 55b, 56b, 57c, 58e, 58f).
  - `c1_freeze_v07` refuses a `q1` entry, an `h8_units` other than `folds`, and a freeze without `data:e0_plan` or `data:scene_json`; it reads nothing else of the config.
  - **Step 19a** (section 8.5), before the freeze and outcome-free (it reads the config JSON only): the lead runs `c1_rules_v02.config_check` (a constant restated with another value, an unknown `criteria` key, or a `c` other than 0.03 is refused), `c1_rules_v02.check_h9_label_c1` (`h9_label` must be "descriptive"), and the shape check v02, `70_TESTS/c1_v01/plan_v07_check/config_shape_check_v02.py` (SHA-256 27f3c4633480438c61ac81102a14591f90a43df1469758f6677c4e2c9046c2b1; Choice 57c), with `--expect-routes within_lt3d,transfer_lt3d,voxelnext_val,public_lt3d_val,source_transfer`, whose log must record the pinned reference's SHA-256 (9e26213e…) as `reference_sha256`; and the value check v02, `70_TESTS/c1_v01/plan_v07_check/config_value_check_v02.py` (SHA-256 eeabe7e8f104c437d2d6a4552b405d9705d9f71ebee2c12f26c701e2a6d3b705; Choices 58e, 59c), which replaces v01 (`70_TESTS/c1_v01/plan_v07_check/config_value_check_v01.py`, e9fbd51c…, kept). All four must pass, and their results go into the freeze-time record.
  - **What shape check v02 checks** (Choice 57c). It compares the config's key tree and value types with the rehearsal's configuration, ignoring values; under `e0.plan_values` and `e0.inputs_sha256` it compares value types only. Two top-level keys are optional: `note` (a string) and `method_stage_descriptive`, which must have the shape of `h9_descriptive` (the AV2 config omits it). Each route object is compared with the rehearsal's route of the same name, so every key it must hold, every key it may not hold and every value's shape are checked, `tab` included; `voxelnext_val`, which the rehearsal lacks, is compared with `public_lt3d_val`, because both run on T1 and T3 only. Route names must be unique and from `ROUTE_FOLDS_ROLE`, and `--expect-routes` requires all five. The lead tested it: it passes the rehearsal's configuration and a five-route configuration with `method_stage_descriptive`, and it catches a wrong type, a missing and an extra key in a route, a duplicate route name, a wrongly shaped `method_stage_descriptive`, and AB3DMOT entries in a route whose model has none. It replaces v01 (`70_TESTS/c1_v01/plan_v07_check/config_shape_check_v01.py`, 492b8785…, kept) and v07i's hand check of the route objects.
  - **What the value check checks** (Choices 58e, 59c; R20 re-check v04, m4; R20 re-check v05, m1). It compares the values that the shape check ignores, and fails on any difference: `e0.seed` with 20261114 and with the E⁰ output's seed; `e0.sigma_a` with 0.10 and with the E⁰ output's primary sigma; `e0.plan_values` with the E⁰ output's `H8_E0_primary` (the same cells and values); `e0.inputs_sha256` with the E⁰ output's; `c` with 0.03 on both trackers; `h9_label` with "descriptive"; the files that `folds`, `folds_val150` and `folds_train225` name with the freeze's `--folds`, `--folds-val150` and `--folds-train225`, by SHA-256; the set of `manifests` with the freeze's `--stageA-manifests`; each route's `folds` file with the freeze's file for its role (`ROUTE_FOLDS_ROLE`: `--folds` for `within_lt3d` and `transfer_lt3d`, `--folds-val150` for the other three), by SHA-256, because a wrong file would make H1 NO-GO with no correction; and `e0.inputs` (workers, gt, meta, horizon) with exactly the inputs the E⁰ output hashed, by path, never opened, because a wrong path would make H8 "not tested". It also requires unique route names, unique (runs folder, tag) pairs and unique output files, and that no output the config names, and no file carrying a route's tag in its runs folder, exists before the freeze. It is outcome-free and opens no ground-truth file. The lead tested it: it passes the rehearsal's configuration with its output-absence test switched off (the rehearsal's outputs exist), reports those outputs with the test on, and catches a wrong seed, a missing plan-value cell and a wrong folds file; v02 also catches a wrong route folds file, a wrong ground-truth path and a dropped worker. Its option `--skip-output-absence` exists for tests only and is never used in C1.
  - `c1_analyse_v10 --c1` repeats `config_check` and the `h9_label` check at the analysis.
- **Stated, not defaulted** (Choice 49j; R20 N11; R17g M-2). Under `--c1`, `c` is required: `c1_analyse_v10` stops without it, and v07h's "code default (0.03)" holds only outside `--c1`. `h9_label` has no default. Both are stated.

### 6.2 Definitions and levels (Choices 50, 52c, 53a, 53b; register C2, C8, C9, C10, C11)

Per tracker t; P ∈ {PD7, SD1_7, PD}; f_{a,l} is arm a's infeasible-decision rate in log l.

```
Levels (one-sided; section 6.5 gives the principle)
  γ_M    = 0.95             evidence against M* or M*Jx: cell, family and pooled-LB flags in Valid and ValidJ;
                            M*'s "not flagged" in H6b and H10; H6a, H7, H9
  γ_rm   = 1 − 0.0125/9     each of a comparator's 9 removal conditions from V_t: the cell flag, the family flag and
                            the pooled lower bound, over PD7, SD1_7 and PD (Choices 51b, 52c); H1's route and the
                            descriptive routes
  γ_rm4  = 1 − 0.0025/9     the same 9 conditions on H4's route, transfer_lt3d (Choice 53b)
  γ_rmJ  = 1 − 0.0025/3     each of a comparator's 3 removal conditions from V^J_t (Choice 52c)
  L      = 0.995            HK1c's and ValidJ's pooled upper bound (calibrated; see below)
  γ_for  = 0.975            evidence for M*: Form C, H10's NI part, and the cell parts of H6b and H10
                            (the comparator's flag and the paired lower bound)

Flag_cell^γ(a,P,c)  := LB^{sup-t,γ}_{a,P}(c) > δ,  or  SE = 0 and Ê_{a,P}(c) > δ
NoFlag^γ(a,P)       := no evaluable cell of P flagged at γ (all-evaluable family)
FamFlag^γ(a,P)      := some cell of a's power-qualified family flagged at γ in that family's own sup-t
NoFlag_pool^γ(a,P)  := pooled cell of P evaluable and LB^{γ}(pooled) ≤ δ
Floor(a,P)          := F_{a,P} ≥ 6 for P ∈ {PD7, SD1_7};  ≥ 1 evaluable cell for P = PD
HK1c(a,P)           := UB^{L}(pooled) ≤ δ + 0.03,  L = 0.995

Valid(a,t)          := AND over P of [ NoFlag^{γ_M} ∧ ¬FamFlag^{γ_M} ∧ NoFlag_pool^{γ_M} ∧ Floor ∧ HK1c ]
ShownAbove^γ(c,t)   := OR over P of [ ¬NoFlag^{γ} ∨ FamFlag^{γ} ∨ (pooled evaluable ∧ ¬NoFlag_pool^{γ}) ]
                       (9 conditions; no HK1c term, Choice 47e)
V_t                 := { c ∈ K : ¬ShownAbove^{γ_rm}(c,t) }   on H1's route  (Choices 9, 47e: a floor-only or HK1c-only failure stays in)
V_t^{(4)}           := { c ∈ K : ¬ShownAbove^{γ_rm4}(c,t) }  on H4's route
Δ_t(c)              := mean over logs of ( f_{M*,l} − f_{c,l} )

FormB(t)            := Valid(M*,t) ∧ ∀ c ∈ V_t : UB^{1 − 0.0125/|V_t|}(Δ_t(c)) < 0      (V_t empty: part (ii) vacuous)
FormA(t)            := Valid(M*,t) ∧ ∀ c ∈ {B0,B1,B4,B9} : UB^{1 − 0.025/4}(Δ_t(c)) < 0  (descriptive; Choice 53a)
FormC(t)            := UB^{γ_for}(Δ_t(B4)) ≤ c_t,   c_T1 = c_T3 = 0.03

U_max(M*,t,P)       := max over evaluable cells c of P of UB^{sup-t,0.95}_{M*,P}(c)    (the upper end of the 0.95 sup-t band
                       whose lower ends decide M*'s flags; descriptive, decides nothing, approximate; Choice 51b)

J_P(a)              := log-level mean share of a's decisions with some relied constraint of P exceeded over the 4
                       calibrated steps and all tracks, at the reference pose and full margin
ValidJ(a,t)         := AND over P of [ pooled joint population evaluable (≥ 39 logs) ∧ LB^{γ_M}(J_P(a)) ≤ δ ∧ UB^{L}(J_P(a)) ≤ δ + 0.03 ]
V^J_t               := { c ∈ K_J : for every P, ¬( pooled joint population evaluable ∧ LB^{γ_rmJ}(J_P(c)) > δ ) }
                       (3 conditions, each at γ_rmJ; imprecision never removes a comparator, Choice 49c)
```

**ShownAbove, HK1c and an asymmetry** (Choices 47e, 49c, 50b, 52c, 53b; R20 P13, the stricter branch). ShownAbove is the condition for leaving V_t, and V^J_t's removal rule plays the same part for H3. Failing HK1c (pooled upper bound above δ + 0.03) can reflect imprecision rather than evidence above δ, so it is not a removal condition, for H1, H3 or H4: a comparator that fails only HK1c stays in, like a floor-only failure, and M\* (or M\*Jx) must still beat it on operability (Choice 9's logic). M\*'s own HK1c requirement, inside Valid, and M\*Jx's ValidJ are unchanged. A comparator leaves only on familywise evidence, at the removal levels above, because the comparison set is selected from the data (section 6.5). The same flag test is applied to every arm, at the level its use calls for. An error that makes flags too easy makes M\* fail more often but removes comparators more often; the decision therefore lists, per route, tracker and comparator, the condition that removed it (cell flag, family flag or pooled lower bound, with its population and the level), or that it stayed in (`V_t_reasons`).
- **Zero-SE removals** (Choice 52g item 5). A comparator cell with zero SE and Ê > δ has the same exceedance in every log, and it removes the comparator on that point estimate, outside the 0.0125 removal budget. Each such removal is reported, and R21 reports H1 with and without these removals; the paper says so.
- **On development** (README v10's development evidence), the stricter comparator rule (Choices 47e and 49c, at the pre-criteria levels, on the rehearsal150_v08 inputs) changes no comparator's V_t membership, no H3 V^J membership and no H1 or H3 status: every comparator that fails HK1c there is also flagged. At this plan's levels (removal at 1 − 0.0125/9, Form B at 1 − 0.0125/|V_t|, B = 40000), the development replay v02 on the K8 v03 inputs gives V_t {B3} on T1 and {B0, B3} on T3, as before, and check v03 gives V^J {B3} on T1 and {B0, B3} on T3.

**HK1c: margin and level** (Choice 50c; register C8).
- **The margin, δ + 0.03, is our own value (class OWN), supported by an approximate derivation.** For split conformal with indicator losses, the realised risk of a correctly calibrated rule follows a Beta(l, n + 1 − l) law (Vovk 2012; Angelopoulos and Bates, arXiv 2107.07511, section 3.2). At n = 300 that law is Beta(15, 286), and δ + 0.03 is its 98.4th percentile. For C1's fraction losses, stratified thresholds and fallback strata the law is an approximation, and the paper says so. It is not a theorem about C1's rule.
- **The level is calibrated** (Loh 1987). At 375 units the 0.95 bootstrap bound covered only 0.915 to 0.920 near δ when pooled (0.898 to 0.934 by population and tracker). **The rule, and its timing.** It was stated in the lead's request to the check's analyst at 13:37 EST on 5 Nov, before the check ran, and its coded form, `50_CODE/c1_criteria/c1crit_rstar_v02.py` (687870d6…; `LEVELS_A` and `BRADLEY_A` at lines 56–57, the selection at lines 171–175), was written after that request and before any result (Choice 52g item 7): L is the smallest of {0.975, 0.99, 0.995} whose development coverage at 375 units, for truth in [0.02, 0.10), is ≥ 0.9625 in each of PD7, SD1_7 and PD on both trackers; 0.9625 is Bradley's liberal limit at α = 0.025; if no level qualifies, L = 0.995.
- **Its open reading.** The rule did not say whether coverage meant the mean over a population's cells or each cell's minimum. The analyst applied the mean: **mean coverage** 0.930 to 0.961 at 0.975, 0.955 to 0.980 at 0.99 and 0.967 to 0.988 at 0.995, so L = 0.995. On per-cell minima no level qualifies (worst 0.949, T3 SD1_7), and the fallback gives the same answer.
- **The boundary-only reading, disclosed.** With every band cell rescaled to truth exactly 0.08, the HK1c boundary, 0.99 would already reach ≥ 0.963 everywhere. The rule is applied as it was stated, because it was fixed before the result, AV2's zero inflation is unknown and the band covers the more zero-inflated cells, and the cost is power, not validity.
- **The cost.** At 375 development units, P(H1 fails | M\* valid at cell level) is 0.067 under this plan's levels (check v03; 0.060 under Choice 50's levels, check v02), against 0.020 at v07d's levels, mostly through HK1c on T1's PD cell (section 7). On development, M\*'s largest pooled UB at 0.995 is 0.0625 (T1 PD; check v02, B = 2000) and 0.0627 at B = 40000 (development replay v02), against 0.08; check v03's note prints 0.063, a rounding of 0.0628 (B = 20000; Choice 54c).

**An empty V_t (or V^J_t) is vacuous (Choice 25; R17c).** If no comparator remains, Form B's part (ii) holds vacuously, so FormB(t) holds whenever Valid(M\*, t) holds. This matches K8 and Choice 1: Form B asks for operability among valid methods, and there is then none to beat. The decision reports the part as "vacuous", never as a rejection. For H1 a vacuous part counts as holding. For a family-S member (H3, H4) a vacuous part gives no p-value: it enters Holm with p = 1, its status is "does not hold" and it is not confirmed (section 6.5); because its bound form (`status_bound_nominal`) says "holds", the decision lists it among the disagreements of `S_decision_forms`, and that listing changes nothing (R17g m-5; Choice 55c).

### 6.3 Hypotheses, decision forms and the GO condition (Choices 33, 52e, 53b)

**One decision form per hypothesis** (Choice 52e). **H1**, outside Holm, is decided by its bounds. **Every member of family S** (H2, H3, H4, H6b, H10) is decided by its p-value, (k + 1)/(B + 1), through Holm at 0.025 (section 6.5); the bound at the level Holm assigns, computed from the same replicates, is reported beside it in the decision's `S_decision_forms` block, with every disagreement between the two (through the bootstrap quantile's interpolation, or by one replicate since Choice 53d) listed and never acted on. H6b's bound is not available, because the method stage records p-values only. With the gate closed, Holm is not run, and the statuses are the bound form's, labelled supportive (section 6.5).

```
H1 (primary) := FormB(T1) ∧ FormB(T3)        within AV2, own detector, 375 logs, decision mode; decided by the bounds
C1 GO        :⇔ H1 holds, with every input H1 needs present and provenance-valid. H1's inputs (R20 m6) are, on the
                within_lt3d route for T1 and for T3: the runs and fits of M*, B0, B1, B3, B4, B6, B9 and RETUNED,
                with the 21 merged α/δ′ points RETUNED selects from; the folds; the split; the stage-A to stage-B chain;
                and the deposit record. Otherwise NO-GO, with H1's failed or missing conditions named (and nothing else).
                A missing secondary or descriptive arm never changes GO.

H2  := FormC(T1) ∧ FormC(T3)
       p_H2 = max over t of (k + 1)/(B + 1), k the replicates with Δ_t(B4) > c_t
H3  := ∀ t: ValidJ(M*Jx,t) ∧ ∀ c ∈ V^J_t : UB^{1 − 0.025/(2|V^J_t|)}(f_{M*Jx} − f_c) < 0   (nominal bound form)
       p_H3 = max over t of min(1, 2·|V^J_t|·max_{c ∈ V^J_t} p_c); a fold whose λ does not qualify fails H3 for that tracker
H4  := H1's formula with every fitted arm from the transfer fits, its comparison set V_t^{(4)} formed at γ_rm4;
       RETUNED's transfer point required; nominal bound UB^{1 − 0.025/(2|V_t^{(4)}|)}(Δ_t(c)) < 0 (the same number as
       1 − 0.0125/|V_t^{(4)}|); p_H4 = max over t of min(1, 2·|V_t^{(4)}|·max_c p_c)
H6a := ∀ t: M* flagged at γ_M in no E1-AV2 cell, fixed-rule and refit-CV
H6b := ∀ t: ∃ E1-AV2 cell c where M* is not flagged at γ_M:
            max( p of B4's sup-t flag (refit-CV), p of the paired sup-t difference Ê_B4(c) − Ê_M*(c) ) ≤ 0.025,
            each p converted to (k + 1)/(refit_B + 1); the p-value form of "B4 flagged at 0.975 and LB^{sup-t,0.975}
            of the paired difference > 0"; p_H6b = max over t of the smallest such per-cell p
H7  := ∀ t: M-stage M*_NS flagged at γ_M in no E2-AV2 cell ∧ pooled E2-AV2 cell LB^{sup-t,0.95} ≤ δ
H8  := T1, on exactly the units of the folds role (375 logs):
       ∃ r ∈ {<20, 20-40}: lateral in-path car cell r has LB > max(δ, E⁰_r) under B0
       ∧ its truth-oriented exceedance has LB > δ
       (X2's FAILS rule; the band is X1's two-sided 95 % sup-t band over the 4 decision cells, `x1_lib_v03.sup_t_band`,
        so each LB is one-sided 0.975; register C12a)
H9  := descriptive (Choice 52b; section 6.4); reported per tracker: the Trajectron++ PD cells' estimates and simultaneous
       one-sided 95 % bounds, the power-qualified E2 family size, the floor (6) and whether it is met
H10 := ∀ t: [ ∃ PD7 cell c evaluable for both: Flag_cell^{0.975}(B6,PD7,c) ∧ ¬Flag_cell^{γ_M}(M*,PD7,c)
                                              ∧ LB^{sup-t,0.975}(Ê_B6(c) − Ê_M*(c)) > 0 ]
            ∧ UB^{0.975}(Δ_t(B6)) ≤ 0.03
       p_H10 = max over t of the larger of the cell part's sup-t p and the NI part's p
```

**E⁰ for H8** (Choice 31, X1's frozen definition): E⁰_r = P(U⁰ > z₀.₉₅ | kept), a plug-in with truth := m̂ over all (record, axis) candidates before the separating filter, 20 perturbation draws (MC SE ≈ 0.0015, reported; register C12b), σ_a = 0.10 m, computed from stage-A fields of the 375 logs (own detector, T1), with the one prepared ground-truth file, before the quarantine and the freeze:

`[SLOT: S21 H8_E0_primary for car|<20|lat and car|20-40|lat, and e0 inputs_sha256]`

The analysis re-hashes the E⁰ inputs, recomputes E⁰ with the same code and seed, and compares bit for bit. After the quarantine it reads the ground-truth input from its quarantine member, through a temporary file outside the workspace, and checks the decompressed SHA-256 against the recorded hash.

**What a missing or unverifiable input does (Choice 33; Choice 31's NO-GO clause is superseded, Choice 38a).**
- An input H1 needs (the `within_lt3d` route of T1 or T3, or H1's own block), missing, not provenance-valid, made under another deposit record, without a "done" line in the run log, or raising an exception from the entry point: **NO-GO**, with the reason, even if a fix exists later (section 9). An environment stop is not an exception (section 9). That includes a resource error that the analysis records here (one of section 9's seven strings in the exception text): it makes the whole decision an environment stop, and the same frozen command reruns under the next output name (Choices 55a, 56a).
- An input or route a secondary claim needs (H2 to H8, H10), missing, not provenance-valid, made under another deposit record, without a "done" line in the run log, or raising an exception: that claim is **"not tested"**, with the reason or the exception text recorded in `missing_inputs`; it enters family S with p = 1 (if it is a family-S member), is never confirmed, and **never changes GO** (Choice 39d, M2). The same holds for H9's descriptive report. A method-stage table that cannot be used makes H6a, H6b and H7 "not tested" together, because the analysis reads the three from it in one block (R17g section 2). A resource error recorded here is an environment stop, not "not tested" (section 9). Only a guard breach (an outcome-blindness or binding violation) aborts the analysis.
- **"Not tested" only for a route without a table of record** (Choice 47h; R20 P6). A route is "not tested" only if it has no "done" line or its run raised an exception that is not an environment stop (section 9); a logged table that exists but was not consumed is a deviation (section 8.6). An output without a "done" line is never a table of record (Choice 49f).
- **Q1** is "not tested" by decision (Choice 44d): the frozen config has no `q1` entry, and the analysis reports Q1 with the two reasons of section 1.3, never among the missing inputs. Q1 has its own block, so it cannot change H9, family S, `gate_H1` or GO (Choice 42f m5).
- **Unit lists and seeds** (Choices 39d M4, 42d). Every table records its sorted unit list and its hash. The method stage and the predictor arm must use exactly the bound folds' units; **H8 exactly the units of the `folds` role** (the frozen config's `h8_units` is `folds`; a table on a subset of the 375 logs is not valid); each table the folds' dataset, the current hash log and the current deposit record. The primary H9 table must come from forward-pass seed 20261102. Under C1, `--scenes` is refused.
- **E⁰ problems make H8 "not tested"**: a missing `e0` entry, missing input hashes, changed or unbound inputs, a recomputed value that differs, or an H8 table without the plan's `E0_plan`. None changes the verdict.
- Every claim gets one status: holds, does not hold, not tested (or descriptive). `gate_H1` equals (verdict = GO), and no claim is confirmed unless the gate holds and the claim holds.

### 6.4 H9: descriptive (Choices 23, 35, 37, 38b, 39b, 42f m1, 43b, 44e, 52b)

- **H9 is descriptive in C1** (S20c; Choice 52b). An outcome-free projection shows that its floor cannot be met at the planned size: `c1_power_counts_v05` part H9, with the target-only power rule and 2000 datasets, on the 150-log public-LT3D stage-A items resampled to 375 logs over 20 draws (`70_TESTS/c1_v01/status_v09/dev_s22_power_counts_v05_rules.json`, 8e2d6e7a…, 5 Nov 14:56), gives a power-qualified E2 family of **2 cells in every draw** (range 2–2) on each tracker, against the floor of 6: P(≥ 6) = 0. The family covers about 58 % (T1) and 63 % (T3) of the items; on the 150 logs as given it has 0 cells; the history-share sensitivity also gives 2 cells; the earlier rule (`k6_analyse_v02.power_family`, 200 datasets) gave a median of 2 (range 2–4). The projection leaves out history length, so it overstates the power. A test that cannot meet its own floor at the planned size cannot support a confirmatory statement (ICH E9 section 3.5).
  - **What this amends:** Choice 47(c), which kept H9 secondary; and D16 v10 Choice 23's and D17 v04's statement that H9 is "the confirmatory test of a deployed learned predictor". The amendment is made before any AV2 outcome, on counts only.
  - **What the code does** (README v10, verbatim, "H9 in C1"):

> With h9_label "descriptive" (the C1 config's value, which `c1_analyse_v10 --c1` requires), H9's status is "descriptive". The decision's H9_descriptive block reports, per tracker, the Trajectron++ PD cells' estimates and simultaneous one-sided 95 % bounds, the power-qualified E2 family size, the floor (6) and whether it is met, and states: "H9 is descriptive (D16 v33 Choice 52b): the decision reports, per tracker, the Trajectron++ PD cells' estimates and simultaneous one-sided 95 % bounds, the power-qualified E2 family size, the floor (6) and whether it is met; no confirmatory claim is made about a learned predictor". The H9_floor block lists family_size_E2, floor and floor_met per tracker. No H9 output says "does not hold".

  - **The secondary-label wording** of Choices 47(c) and 49(l), "does not hold: floor not met", stays in the code but is not used in C1. At the planned size the floor is expected not to be met (`floor_met` false on both trackers); with the descriptive label this is reported and changes nothing.
  - **When H9's table is missing, not provenance-valid or raises** (R17g m-4; Choice 55c). H9's status is then absent or "not tested", and the decision's `H9_floor` rule text (and, if the block raises, H9's label) carries the code's stock wording of the secondary label, which mentions "does not hold: floor not met". The report then reads **"H9 descriptive: not computed"**, with the reason, whatever that stock text says; on no path does an H9 status say "does not hold".
  - **What the paper states:** the projection, and that the study makes **no confirmatory claim about a learned predictor**. H9 was in no family and does not enter GO; nothing else changes.
- **Population (Choice 35).** PD: the K8 relied in-path items at 2 s, **scored on the K8 tracker-planner's constraints** (the relied axis, the gap and the toward-ego normal and sign taken from the K8 items, which evaluate them at the reference pose, the logged pose at t_k + h), not recomputed from Trajectron++'s own mean. The items are paired with the tracker arm item for item, joined by (log, track, k). **This corrects v04 section 11.4**, which described H9's items as the method-stage in-path audit population; that population serves H6 to H8 only.
- **One keys file.** `c1_predarm_v07` needs one Trajectron++ keys file whose units are exactly the 375 fold units (one stage-B cache for the 375 logs, or keys built over both caches); fold units without keys are refused.
- **Errors.** A unit of the keys outside the folds is an error, before anything is computed; a non-finite D or variance in a used item is an error.
- **History length (Choice 37; register C13a).** Each item's history length is its predictor's own input history at decision time k, read from the Trajectron++ keys (`rec_hist_stratum_trk`: contiguous tracker states up to k, written by `tpp_keys_v03`, which keeps v02's key code); strata {2, 3–4, ≥ 5 states} (category-conditional calibration, Vovk 2012; Monti et al. 2022). Nothing after k is read. A used item below 2 states, or keys without the field, is an error. The cut-points were chosen after D17's outcomes; AV2 is new data.
- **Fit.** DP-SCRC on L0 × HL, L1 × HL, L2 × HL, L3 × HL, then L3 as the last fallback, so every item has a threshold; a cell gets its own exact-CRC threshold with at least 19 calibration units, otherwise its items fall back; cross-fitted on the C1 folds.
- **What is computed and reported** (Choices 39b, 42f m1, 43b; the predictor arm's H9 rule in `c1_predarm_v07`): the power-qualified E2 family by the target-only power rule (`c1_rules_v01.power_target`, 2000 datasets) in the joint evaluable E2 + HL family; the floor of 6 power-qualified E2 cells and whether it is met; E2 cells flagged against the E2-only sup-t families and HL cells against the joint E2 + HL families, at 0.95; whether each HL cell is evaluable; the pooled cell (each item once). Seeds [20261110, i, 5] and [20261111, i, 5]. The predictor arm keeps B = 2000, and its p-values are not converted; no H9 output enters Holm.
- **Seed robustness (Choice 38b).** `c1_predarm_v07` takes the three forward-pass runs (20261102, 20261104, 20261105) in one call, asserts each run's summary seed, and under the plan requires all three before any H9 output is computed. The label is descriptive (`H9_seed_robustness`); it never changes family S or GO. No seed is promoted after outcomes.
- **Trajectron++ environments (Choices 44e, 45a).** Each wrapper is checked against the environment it is meant to run in, and both run **on the laptop**. `tpp_keys_v03` runs under **canmot310**, the environment its builder names, and its wrapper record (python, numpy) is compared with FROZEN_ENV. `tpp_run_v03` runs under **tpp38**, and its wrapper record (python, numpy, torch) is compared with FROZEN_ENV_TPP. Before any key or run is made, `c1_tpp_envcheck_v01` (entry `tpp_envcheck`, outcome-free) writes a dry wrapper record under each interpreter and checks it against the frozen plan. Under the plan, `c1_predarm_v07` requires both blocks and makes both comparisons; a mismatch makes H9 "not tested". **No pod is used** (Choice 60b, which withdraws Choice 45a's pod clause): the keys and runs execute on this laptop, like every post-freeze step (section 9), because `tpp_run` reads the cache from the absolute path recorded in the keys and step 28's frozen command has no `--cache`, so a run made on a pod could not be read back.
- **Why history length.** On the frozen nuScenes D17 run, items with only two tracker states stayed above δ after M\* (0.079, lower bound 0.062, on T1; 0.072 [0.063] on T3), offset by items with five or more (Choice 37, from R19). History length is observable at decision time, so stratifying on it is the method's own principle. It was found on development data and fixed before the freeze.
- **Descriptive companions.** H9-noHL (D17-style strata, E2 cells only, on `k6_analyse_v02.power_family`); the diagnostic `noHL_on_HL_cells`; H9-T; TPPgt. Q1 (QCNet) is not tested (section 1.3).

### 6.5 Multiplicity (Choices 26, 33, 47g, 50a, 50b, 52c, 53b; R20 P4)

- **The principle** (Choice 50a; ICH E9 section 5.5). **Evidence against M\*** is judged at one-sided 0.05, the strict direction for M\*: the cell, family and pooled-LB flags, H6a and H7. **Evidence for M\*** is judged at one-sided 0.025, which equals the conventional two-sided 95 %: H1 as a whole (below); Form C and H10's NI part at UB^{0.975}; the cell parts of H6b and H10 (the comparator's flag and the paired lower bound) at 0.975; Holm over family S at 0.025; and Form A (descriptive) at 0.025/4. **The exception is HK1c:** its level is calibrated rather than set at 0.975, so that its **mean** development coverage meets Bradley's liberal limit for 0.025, which allows an actual error of up to 0.0375 (section 6.2; the worst single development cell was 0.949).
- **H1 totals one-sided 0.025** (Choice 52c; R20 re-check v02, M-2). A false H1 needs either a valid comparator removed from V_t, or superiority wrongly concluded over a kept one, and these two errors add (Bonferroni; Benjamini and Yekutieli 2005 for a data-selected set). So the budget is split: each of the 9 removal conditions is tested at 1 − 0.0125/9, and Form B at UB^{1 − 0.0125/|V_t|}(Δ) < 0. **The nominal total is 0.0125 + 0.0125 = 0.025**, whatever |V_t| is, excluding removals by the zero-SE rule, which are each reported (section 6.2). **HK1c** (nominal 0.005) is a separate component of the intersection-union test, so it enters by the maximum, not by the sum (Berger 1982). **Where HK1c's null holds, H1's actual false-pass probability is governed by HK1c's actual error, not by 0.025:** on development 0.012 to 0.033 on population means and about 0.05 in the worst single cell (section 6.9, item 13; register v05 section 5, item 12; Choice 56d item 5). On development, a comparator planted at δ was removed with probability 0.09 to 0.14 under the 0.95-per-condition rule and ≤ 0.018 at 0.05/9; 0.0125/9 is stricter still.
- **H1 is an intersection-union test** over trackers and conditions. An intersection-union test needs each component only at its level (Berger 1982); the Bonferroni split across V_t (D14 section 4) is kept as a deliberate, conservative choice.
- **H3 and H4, inside family S** (Choices 52c, 53b). Each has a data-selected comparison set. Its removal conditions are tested at half the smallest Holm level, 0.025/5 = 0.005: V^J's 3 conditions at 1 − 0.0025/3, and H4's 9 conditions at 1 − 0.0025/9. Its p-value is doubled before Holm, so P(p ≤ α) ≤ 0.0025 + α/2 ≤ α for every Holm level α ≥ 0.005: each is a nominally valid p-value for Holm (the bootstrap p_c is itself valid only approximately; section 6.1). H1's own V_t is unchanged by H4's level.
- **No-flag validity tests are not corrected across populations or claims;** within a population the sup-t family is simultaneous (R20 m5; register A2: a correction would make a non-rejection claim easier). This covers H6a, H7 and the validity parts of H1, H3 and H4; each population at γ_M.
- **Family S = {H2, H3, H4, H6b, H10}, m = 5 fixed.** Tested only if H1 holds; then **Holm at family-wise one-sided 0.025** (Holm 1979; Dmitrienko and Tamhane 2007; ICH E9 section 5.6), on the p-values of section 6.1. A member that cannot be tested (gate failed, route absent, input missing, no p-value) enters with p = 1. A vacuous Form B part gives no p-value and enters with p = 1, with status "does not hold", listed as a disagreement of the decision forms (section 6.2). A member is confirmed only if Holm rejects it at its level (Choice 52e). If H1 fails, the members are reported unadjusted, in their bound form, as "supportive, not confirmatory (gate H1 not passed)".
- **Family P = {H8}** has its own level, not gated on H1: X2's two-sided 95 % band, that is one-sided 0.025 per bound. **Why no adjustment** (register v05, C11b; ICH E9 section 5.6): H8 replicates a premise about B0, not a claim about M\*.
- **Study-wise** (register v05, C11b; Choice 54c): {H1 → S} by serial gatekeeping (H1 at 0.025; S under Holm at 0.025 only if H1 holds) controls its error at one-sided 0.025 nominal (Dmitrienko and Tamhane 2007); with P at its own 0.025, every confirmatory statement together is at most 0.05 nominal.
- **H9** is descriptive and belongs to no family (Choice 52b).
- **Outside every family:** every descriptive result, including H9, AB3DMOT's rows, H9's seed-robustness label, M\*'s descriptive upper bounds and the criteria audit's outputs; and Q1, which is not tested. The code passes only T1 and T3 to the decision, so AB3DMOT cannot reach `gate_H1` or family S (Choice 38c).

### 6.6 Reading rules (E8, E9, E14; R16)

- **Loop mode (E8).** Descriptive only; no ordering of arms; T1 is never quoted without T3. LOG's zero holds by construction (the artefact filter removes every collision LOG has at the same scene and delay) and LOG is never a reference. Allowed sentence: "In closed-loop log replay with non-reactive agents, no difference in at-fault collisions between M\* and any comparator was resolved on either tracker."
- **V2 (E9).** Reported at its development level with the dependence stated: M\*'s V2 is 0.358 [0.325, 0.392] (T1) and 0.351 [0.320, 0.386] (T3), against the track reference 0.308; a decision holds about 8.35 tracks but 30 relied records. The per-constraint certificate leaves about one decision in three with some margin broken; M\*Jx (H3) is the pre-registered remedy.
- **RETUNED (E14).** Selected by the pooled rule, judged by the cell rule; reported with the arm chosen per fold and whether any α/δ′ point passes Form B's rule in sample. RETUNED-HK1, cross-fitted on Form B's rule, is descriptive and never in K.
- **Validity in the paper** (Choice 52f; R20 re-check v02, M-4). The paper's sentence is, with k, x, y and z from the decision (S20a's families at the planned size; the decision's `Mstar_power_families` block):

  > "No cell was shown above δ. Each of the k power-qualified cells (covering x % of PD7, y % of SD1_7 and z % of PD records) had power ≥ 0.8 to be flagged at a per-cell exceedance of 0.15. The other evaluable cells were tested without a power claim."

  The descriptive upper bounds are reported beside it, labelled approximate.
- **H9 in the paper:** descriptive, with the projection of section 6.4 and the statement that no confirmatory claim is made about a learned predictor.
- **H6b in the paper:** its p-value can reach Holm's smallest level only with k = 0 (section 6.1).
- **The 12 overclaim sentences of R16 section 5 bind the write-up.**
- **The outcome-blindness statement** is made only in the scoped form of section 11.3.

### 6.7 H3's caveats (Choice 21)

Point estimates just under δ, validity resting on the +0.03 tolerance; certification at the reference pose and full margin, the executed-margin joint exceedance not certified; decision mode only; the held-out joint loss varied by fold (0.030 to 0.075 on development), so the guarantee holds in expectation. With λ fitted on 75 logs the CRC allows a calibration-fold joint loss of at most 0.037 (0.044 on nuScenes folds), so λ and the price will sit somewhat above development's (+0.34 on T1, +0.42 on T3). On development (check v03), M\*Jx's ValidJ upper bounds at 0.995 are PD7 0.056 / 0.060, SD1_7 0.011 / 0.013 and PD 0.005 / 0.008 (T1 / T3), all ≤ 0.08; V^J at 1 − 0.0025/3 is {B3} on T1 and {B0, B3} on T3; H3 passes at every Holm level, and its bounds at the Holm-assigned levels, UB^{1 − α/(2|V^J|)}, are −0.367 to −0.377 (T1, B3), −0.151 to −0.158 (T3, B0) and −0.254 to −0.260 (T3, B3); the bound and p readings agree at every level.

### 6.8 Sources of the design choices (Choices 47l, 50i, 54b, 56c; register v05 sections 2 and 4)

**The full basis table** is `90_DECISIONS/C1_CRITERIA_REGISTER_v05.md` (SHA-256 c2e8d6b79290424b10d15c5cdf239797311ce393c833a9337541b8bf0b6a5d51): all 46 criteria with their decided value, basis, class (PROVEN, STANDARD, PRACTICE, DEV, OWN) and confidence, brought up to Choices 52 and 53 (rows C3b, C6e, C9b, C9c, C11a, C11b, C13a and C13b, and the principle's selection budget; Choice 54b) and to R20's re-check v03 (Choice 56c): every operating figure is labelled with its check (check v02 at Choice 50's levels, check v03 at the final levels); C3b's p-value statement is narrowed to "never 0 and conservative for the Monte Carlo step at any B; the bootstrap p-value itself is valid only approximately"; Form B's smallest tail count is stated; and the limit on H1's actual error is added (its section 5, item 12). No value changes. The plan's levels (sections 6.2 and 6.5: C9b, C9c, C11a, C11b), B and the p-value rule (section 6.1: C3b, C6e) and H9's status (section 6.4: C13a, C13b) match those rows. Registers v04 (470743a9…) and v03 (8d2be6c5…) are kept as the records of Choices 54 and 50. Bian and Barber 2023 and Bao et al. 2024 were checked on the web on 5 Nov 2026; Loh 1987, Browne 1995 and Turner, Prevost and Thompson 2004 were checked at abstract level on 5 Nov; the other entries are as R20, the evidence review (`90_DECISIONS/C1_CRITERIA_EVIDENCE_REVIEW_v01.md`, d24bfe63…, section 7), register v05's section 4 (unchanged from v04) and D16 v34 to v37 give them, and are checked again before the manuscript cites them. R20's novelty-risk list (R20 v01, "Literature gaps (b)") belongs to the manuscript and is carried in D16 v31 Choice 49(k).

### 6.9 Limits, as the paper states them (register v05 section 5; Choices 52, 53, 56)

1. **The detectable effect.** Our power rule gives each power-qualified cell power 0.80 against an excess of 0.15, an anchor taken from comparator cells that were flagged, and so selected for size. On the 840 development scenes the SD1_7 floor fails at 0.10, and at 375 units with development record counts it fails at 0.12. We therefore claim validity against per-cell excesses of about 0.15 and above, not smaller ones; we claim power only for the power-qualified cells; and we report MDE80 per cell.
2. **HK1c's coverage is approximate.** At L = 0.995 its mean development coverage is 0.967 to 0.988, and the worst single cell is 0.949. The Beta-law margin is exact for split conformal with indicator losses; for our fraction losses and fallback strata it is an approximation.
3. **U_max and the cell upper bounds are approximate.** Their development coverage near δ is 0.78 at 39 units and 0.87 at 150.
4. **Three paired bounds under-cover slightly:** B3 at 0.975 (0.9678 on T1, 0.9725 on T3) and at 0.9875 (0.9835 on T1, 0.9815 on T3), and B0 on T3 at 0.975 (0.9728). All are above Bradley's lower limit of 0.9625, and every Form B bound is far from 0 (−0.722 on T1; −0.565 and −0.677 on T3, at this plan's levels, from the development replay v02; register v05 labels its figures: −0.725, −0.571 and −0.680 from check v02 at Choice 50's levels, and −0.722, −0.566 and −0.677 from check v03 at the final levels).
5. **Zero-SE removals** remove a comparator on a point estimate, outside the removal budget; each is reported, with H1 with and without them.
6. **H6b's resolution.** Its p-value comes from 200 refit-CV replicates, so it can pass at Holm's smallest level only with k = 0; the cost is power.
7. **H9 is descriptive.** An outcome-free projection showed that its floor cannot be met at the planned size, so we make no confirmatory claim about a learned predictor.
8. **H3 and H4 have no development replay.** Development has no M\*J or transfer arm, so their rules are tested on constructed cases and in the rehearsal (register v05 section 5, item 11).
9. **Some values are our own judgement:** F_MIN 6 and PD ≥ 1; MIN_UNITS 39; HK1c's margin of 0.03; c = 0.03 for Form C and H10; B's rule of 10 replicates beyond every decision quantile; H9's floor; E⁰'s 20 draws; σ_a = 0.10 m; the planner's buffer, range, candidates and v_max; the zero-SE rule, the method stage's repetitions, and the rule that a missing H1 input gives NO-GO. Over its plausible range none of them changes the development verdict, or the value cannot affect GO. The planner sensitivities are descriptive.
10. **All development evidence is nuScenes.** AV2's record counts, zero inflation and within-log correlation may differ. The counts (S20a, S22) are computed from AV2 inputs before stage B, without outcomes, and the frozen rules apply to them as written.
11. **The confidence grades are judgement** about a referee, not a measurement.
12. **Some sources were checked only partly:** some bibliographically or at abstract level only; FDA 2016's text was not opened; Bradley's band comes from secondary sources; the clauses of ISO 21448 and ICH E10 were not read in the primary documents.
13. **H1's actual error** (register v05 section 5, item 12; Choice 56d item 5; R20 re-check v03, m7). Its selection-and-superiority part is 0.025 nominal. Its HK1c part is a separate component of the intersection-union test; at L = 0.995 HK1c's development coverage is 0.967 to 0.988 on population means (an actual error of 0.012 to 0.033) and 0.949 in the worst single cell (about 0.05). Where HK1c's null holds, H1's actual error can therefore exceed 0.025.

## 7. Power and operating characteristics

`[SLOT: S22 power on the real counts]`

**S22 is reported only** (Choices 47i, 49g, 52g item 4). It changes no label, rule, threshold or population. Its tool is `c1_power_counts_v05` (bound builder; its hash is recorded before it runs), with development inputs from the K8 Linux replicate's v03 analysis (R16), and its output is bound as `data:s22`. Part H9 uses the target-only power rule with 2000 datasets on H9's tested population, the Trajectron++ PD items, from the stage-A counts-only items, and **leaves out history length**, so it overstates H9's power. Parts A, B and J keep v02's analytic power family (each cell's normal power at P against the Šidák critical value of its evaluable family), which equals the simulated family for M\* on development (check v02, part D); part B uses the stricter comparator rules (Choices 47e, 49c), and H3's set is not modelled.

**Development operating characteristics at 375 units** (Choices 52c, 52d; check v03, part D3; nuScenes development record counts, 375 development scenes per set, 300 sets per variant, B = 20000; replaced for AV2 by S22). Check v03 ran at B = 20000, and Choice 53's changes do not affect H1: H4's removal level and doubled p apply to H4's route only, and (k + 1)/(B + 1) does not change H1's bound-form decision.

| | Plug-in rates | M\* capped at δ (valid at cell level) |
|---|---|---|
| **P(H1)** | **0.870** | **0.933** |
| P(H1 fails) | 0.130 | 0.067 |
| T1 failure reasons | HK1c 0.103, pooled 0.010, family flag 0.010 | HK1c 0.060, pooled 0.013 |
| T3 failure reasons | family flag 0.023, cell flag 0.017, HK1c 0.003 | family flag 0.003, HK1c 0.003 |
| Kept in V_t, T1 | B1 0.160, B9 0.157, B0 and RETUNED 0.010 | B1 0.143, B9 0.147, B0 and RETUNED 0.027 |
| Kept in V_t, T3 | B9 0.120 (B0 and B3 always) | B9 0.093 |
| Superiority over a kept comparator | holds in every set | holds in every set |
| P(H2), P(H10) | 1.00, 1.00 | 1.00, 1.00 |

- **P(H1 fails | M\* valid at cell level) is 0.067,** against 0.060 under Choice 50's levels (check v02) and 0.020 at v07d's levels, mostly through HK1c on T1's PD cell: M\*'s pooled PD exceedance on T1 is 0.049, close to δ. Both P(H1) figures stay above 0.80. Every failure of Form B comes from M\*'s validity, never from superiority.
- **B1 and B9 stay in V_t more often** than under Choice 50's levels (14 to 16 % of sets on T1, 9 to 12 % on T3, against 2 to 5 %), through the stricter removal level and the larger B; on development M\* is 0.22 to 0.33 less often infeasible than either, so superiority held in every set where one was kept.
- **Stability** (check v03, part E3). In 100 independent seed sets per tracker at B = 20000, V_t and V^J membership were identical to the primary result in **100 of 100**; Valid(M\*), Form B and H10 held in every set; the MC SE of every decisive bound was at most 0.0006.
- **M\*'s families at 375 units**, median (10th percentile), with the record share:

| | PD7 | SD1_7 | PD |
|---|---|---|---|
| P = 0.15 (check v03) | 23 (22–23); share 0.987–0.988 | 11 (9–10); share 0.90–0.91 (p10 0.87–0.88); P(≥ 6) = 1.00 | 2–4 |
| P = 0.12 (check v02) | 18 (17–18); share 0.95 | 3 (2); share 0.57–0.58; P(≥ 6) = 0 | 0 |

- **A stated limit, not a footnote.** At P = 0.12 the SD1_7 floor fails in every development set (family median 3, against 11 at 0.15), so **the study is powered for per-cell excesses of about 0.15**. With 375 units per cell the power curve is steep between 0.12 and 0.15 (on the full 840 scenes the family at 0.12 was 12 to 15). This uses development record counts, not AV2's (section 6.9). The P = 0.12 figures come from check v02; the power rule is the same under this plan's levels.
- **The OC uses the analytic family**, in which target-only and all-cells planting coincide and the dataset count has no role; on the 840 scenes it equals the simulated family for M\* in all six combinations (27/18/5 and 26/18/5), and differs by 0 to 2 cells for B0 and B3.
- **Erratum** (Choice 53a). Check v03's Form A values were computed at 1 − 0.05/4, not at this plan's 1 − 0.025/4; Form A is descriptive, and no verdict depends on it. At 1 − 0.025/4 the development replay v02 gives Form A met on both trackers (T1: B0 −0.1937, B1 −0.2399, B4 −0.0615, B9 −0.2909; T3: B0 −0.5653, B1 −0.1803, B4 −0.0305, B9 −0.2290).
- **These figures supersede** v07d's provisional values (`70_TESTS/c1_scratch/c1_power_375_v04.json`: P(H1) 0.91, 0.97 capped), v07e's (0.870, 0.940) and D18 v03's (P(H1) 0.94; P(B6 passes on T3) 0.02), which used earlier levels (R20 m4). v05's "a family of 6 to 7 cells" for H9 was computed on the method-stage in-path audit population and is withdrawn; H9's projection is in section 6.4.

**Descriptive outputs of the criteria audit** (Choice 50f; they decide nothing), reported with S20a and S22 before the freeze and, where they need outcomes, at analysis (`Mstar_power_families`, `comparator_power_families`, `zero_se_effects`, `Mstar_simultaneous_upper_bounds`):
- the record coverage of each power family;
- the families at F_MIN 4 and 8;
- per-cell power at P = 0.10, 0.12 and 0.20 (500 datasets; 2000 at 0.15), from which MDE80 (the per-cell excess detected with power 0.80) is read;
- every zero-SE case and its effect on V_t;
- U_max and the cell UBs, labelled "approximate" (development coverage near δ 0.78 at 39 units, 0.87 at 150).

The power tools `c1_power_counts_v01` to `v04` are bound only as modules of `c1_power_counts_v05`; their earlier outputs are cited by hash.

| `data:s22` | the S22 power file |
| `data:logs375` | `30_DATA/av2_trk_v02/C1_LOGS_375_v01.json` |
| `data:gt_inventory` | `40_PROTOCOL/C1_GT_INVENTORY_v03.json`, which step 16 always writes, with 0 pending entries (Choices 55c, 56d item 3) |
| `data:rule0_metrics` | the Rule-0 metric files of the 375 logs (`60_RESULTS/av2_trk/c1_375/metrics/`, `hota/`) |
| `data:provenance` | the success record written by `c1_av2_provenance_v01` |
| `data:port_equiv` | the port's equivalence records (`60_RESULTS/av2_trk/c1_375/equiv/`), one `port_equiv=` per record |
| `data:freeze_record` | `40_PROTOCOL/C1_FREEZE_TIME_RECORD_v01.md`, the freeze-time record (Choice 51a; section 10). **Required on AV2** by `c1_freeze_v07`: an AV2 freeze without it is refused. The lead's check in step 19's dry-run listing stays as a second check |
| `data:run_commands` | `40_PROTOCOL/C1_RUN_COMMANDS_v01.txt`, the frozen command list of steps 22 to 29 (section 8.5, step 19b; Choice 58a). Required by this plan, not by the code: the lead checks in step 20's listing that it is bound |
| `data:mstage_train` | the method stage's 76 nuScenes training files, listed below, one `mstage_train=` per file (Choices 59a, 60d) |

`data:s20c` is dropped (Choice 47c).

**The method stage's 76 nuScenes training files** (`data:mstage_train`; Choices 59a, 60d; R20 re-check v05, M-1; R20 re-check v06, notes). `c1_mstage_v06` passes every input to `check_inputs` (`c1_guard_v03`), which accepts only a file in FROZEN_HASHES or an input of a bound stage-A manifest, so without these bindings H6a, H6b and H7 would be "not tested" after the deposit. Files 1 to 28 are exactly the `--train-*` inputs of step 25 that `c1_mstage_v06.input_files` passes to `check_inputs` (each training worker's `x1_tracks.jsonl.gz`, the training ground truth, the `scene.json` of the training meta folder and the horizon). Files 29 to 76 are each training worker's `x1_export_record.json` and `x1_per_frame.json`, which the method stage also reads from the worker folders and the frozen M plan also binds; they are bound so that the C1 binding is complete (Choice 60d). Every SHA-256 is the one that the frozen M plan's hash list gives (`40_PROTOCOL/M_PREREGISTRATION_v05.md`); the author re-hashed all 76 on 6 Nov 2026, and each equals its listed value. Files 1 to 12, 27 and 29 to 52 are T1's; 13 to 24, 28 and 53 to 76 are T3's; 25 and 26 are shared.
