**The bound stage-A manifests, by role** (`stageA_manifest`; their paths and hashes are recorded in FROZEN_HASHES; every stage-A manifest stays in place until stage B, Choice 48a):
- route `within_lt3d` (and `transfer_lt3d`, `source_transfer`): the 375-log own-detector manifests of T1, T3 and AB3DMOT (`tab`), built by `av2_cache_stageA_v02` from the port's inputs (README v10: the one ground-truth file, the ego file `30_DATA/av2_trk_v02/ego/av2_c1_ego10hz_v01.json.gz`, and each tracker's exports and horizon file under `30_DATA/av2_trk_v02/`);
- route `public_lt3d_val` (H1-P): `30_DATA/av2_cache_stageA_v01/cache_stageA_t1_lt3d/` and `cache_stageA_t3_lt3d/`;
- route `voxelnext_val` (H5): `30_DATA/av2_cache_stageA_v01/cache_stageA_t1_voxelnext/` and `cache_stageA_t3_voxelnext/`.

`30_DATA/av2_cache_stageA_v01/cache_stageA_ab3dmot_lt3d/` serves no route; the quarantine and the freeze find it with every other AV2 stage-A manifest under `30_DATA`.

**The provenance line** (Choices 46d, 48b m-2, 52g item 2; the only post-lock change outside the slots). Provenance record SHA-256: `<written here at the freeze: the SHA-256 of the data:provenance file>`. The record is the success record of `c1_av2_provenance_v01 --readme 50_CODE/c1_av2/README_C1_CODE_v10.md`, bound as `data:provenance`. For every AV2 stage-A manifest to bind, the tool requires `builder_sha256` to be a bound builder; follows each tracker export's receipt and its detection input's receipt to bound builders and adapters; and re-makes the files that carry no receipt (the T1 and T3 horizons, the ego file and the ground truth, each hashed and deleted unopened) with the bound builders outside the workspace, comparing hashes; the AB3DMOT horizon is checked as the worker-order concatenation of the bound AB3DMOT builder's worker files. It writes its record only if every check passes, and exits non-zero otherwise. `c1_freeze_v07` refuses a `data:provenance` file that is not a success record of that tool, made for **README v10**, with every check, covering every AV2 stage-A manifest passed. The freeze itself checks only the stage-A manifests' own `builder_sha256` (and refuses an AV2 stage-A manifest without one, m-6); the upstream builders are covered by the provenance check alone. **The freeze does not compare the record's own `tool_sha256` with the bound tool**; the lead does, at step 19 (R17g m-3; Choice 55c). **A cosmetic point** (R17g m-9): the record's `kinds` field lists `scene_json` even for the v01-built 150-log manifests, which record no scene map; each manifest's own entry shows that the check was skipped.

**The blocks at the end of this file are written by the freeze, not by hand (Choice 41a).** The draft carries only the empty FROZEN_HASHES and FROZEN_ROLES blocks. `c1_freeze_v07` fills those two and inserts three more after them, in this order:
- FROZEN_ENV: the Python, NumPy and SciPy versions of canmot310, asserted at run time by the guard, and compared with the `tpp_keys_v03` wrapper records (Choice 44e);
- FROZEN_ENV_TPP: the python, numpy and torch versions of the `tpp38` environment, read by running its interpreter or given by `--tpp-env`, and compared with the `tpp_run_v03` wrapper records (Choices 42f m4, 44e);
- FROZEN_TESTS: `test_c1_v13`'s hash, verdict and output hash, run with `C1_FREEZE_LOG_SHA256`; the generator checks; the Choice 36 edit checks; the stage-A builder comparison; the quarantine check and the workspace hash scan, with the skipped folders listed by name (section 8.4); the frozen-config check; the scene-map check; and the provenance-record check.

`c1_freeze_v07` refuses a plan that already holds a FROZEN_ENV, FROZEN_ENV_TPP or FROZEN_TESTS block, and asserts that each of the five blocks appears exactly once in the frozen file (Choice 41a; it keeps v05's check and block layout).

### 8.2 One freeze, the stage-B log, and amendments (Choices 39c, 42a, 42c, 44c, 48)

- **One freeze.** The plan is frozen once, by `c1_freeze_v07`. It refuses if the canonical log `40_PROTOCOL/C1_PLAN_HASH_LOG.txt` already holds a C1 line, unless `--amend "<reason>"` is given.
- **No freeze and no amendment after an outcome run.** The freeze refuses **any** freeze or amendment while the append-only stage-B log `40_PROTOCOL/C1_STAGE_B_LOG.txt` exists, while any stage-B manifest is found under `30_DATA` (the depth-limited folder search, depth ≤ 3, stays as an extra check), while the run log holds a line from a role other than `entry:c1_e0`, `entry:c1_folds` and `entry:c1_freeze`, or while the run log's chain is broken. The stage-B log and the run log, not a folder search, are the record (Choices 42c, 44c).
- **When `--amend` is refused** (Choice 44c). In every case above, and also if the deposit record exists or any `30_DATA/av2_cache_stageB_*` folder exists. So deleting the deposit record does not reopen amendments once a method-stage, H8 or other outcome run has been logged. In practice an amendment is possible only between the freeze and the deposit record, before any non-exempt run.
- **The remaining limit** (Choices 44c, 48b m-4; stated again in section 8.8). Deleting or truncating the run log (cutting it back after its last exempt line) as well would still reopen `--amend` in code. What then remains is the second OSF registration and the deviation log.
- **Amendments** are logged in `40_PROTOCOL/C1_DEVIATION_LOG_v01.md` with their reason and appended to the hash log as an AMENDED line. The guard accepts only the **latest** line of the log, so an amendment replaces the plan; every output records the log's hash. The deposit record must name the latest line, and its registration time must be later than that line (section 8.3), so an amended plan needs its own registration.
- **Tests at the freeze.** The freeze runs the bound test (`test_c1_v13`, on README v10) with `C1_FREEZE_LOG_SHA256` set to the hash of the log it freezes into (`absent` for the first freeze), and writes the value into FROZEN_TESTS. The suite's `freeze_skip()` relaxes only the "hash log absent" assertion and the earlier suites' real-log groups; the suite always asserts that there is no deposit record, no stage-B log and no non-exempt run-log line (Choice 44c). The quarantine folder may exist, because it precedes the freeze. Run directly after the freeze, the suites fail their real-log checks by design; FROZEN_TESTS is the record, and it can be reproduced in a copy tree (README v07 m7).
- **The freeze also refuses** (Choices 44a, 48a, 48b, 51a): any of the ground-truth conditions of section 8.4; an AV2 stage-A manifest without `builder_sha256`, or one whose recorded `builder_sha256` is not a bound builder (R17c m8; R17f m-6); a `data:provenance` file that is not a success record for README v10 (m-2); an AV2 freeze without any of its required data roles, `data:freeze_record` included; a missing `entry:c1_quarantine` or `entry:tpp_envcheck`; a config with a `q1` entry or without `h8_units` = `folds`; a bound test that does not print ALL_PASS; a generator that does not reproduce its target (checked on the archived originals where a Choice 36 edit was made); an entry point that is not the latest version; a child fold file that is not a restriction of the parent; an AV2 freeze without `data:scene_json` and `data:e0_plan`, or a scene map that does not give every fold unit its stage-A log; a plan already holding a FROZEN_ENV, FROZEN_ENV_TPP or FROZEN_TESTS block; or an open slot. The freeze does not run until the config checks of step 19a pass (section 6.1).
- **A dry run first** (Choice 48c). After the quarantine, the repeat scan and the freeze-time record, the lead runs `c1_freeze_v07 --dry-run` on the real tree (every check except the bound tests; it writes nothing), makes the two hand checks of step 19 and the config checks of step 19a (section 8.5), and lists the quarantine folder (names only) just before the freeze.
- **What it writes:** the five blocks, the `.sha256` sidecar, and one line in the canonical log.

### 8.3 External deposit before every AV2 outcome (Choices 40, 41b, 42a, 42f m2, 42h, 44f m2, 47j, 49h, 51a)

- **What is deposited.** After the freeze and before stage B, the researcher registers the frozen plan on OSF under embargo, on their own account, in the private project https://osf.io/eumj8/ (created 4 Nov 2026; `40_PROTOCOL/OSF_PROJECT_RECORD_v01.txt`). The upload folder `40_PROTOCOL/OSF_UPLOAD_C1_FROZEN/` holds: the frozen plan `C1_AV2_CONFIRMATORY_PREREG_vNN.md`; its `.sha256` sidecar; the C1 hash-log line; README v10 with the bound-file list and hashes; the folds, split and transfer-fit hash files; **a zip of every bound code file, the frozen config, the data-role files, the GT inventory and `30_DATA/av2_trk_v02/GT_FILES_FOR_QUARANTINE_v01.txt`** (Choices 46c, 47j), except the 76 `mstage_train` files, which are deposited by path and SHA-256 only (section 8.1) and are listed in `SHA256SUMS.txt` (Choice 60d): they are about 3.1 GB of nuScenes-derived files that the frozen M plan v05 already binds; they stay unchanged in the workspace until the results review; they can be rebuilt from nuScenes v1.0-trainval with the M study's bound builders; and no copy is deposited, so nuScenes' licence is not relied on for one; the freeze-time record `C1_FREEZE_TIME_RECORD_v01.md` (Choice 51a); the command list `C1_RUN_COMMANDS_v01.txt` (Choice 58a); the shape check's reference configuration `70_TESTS/c1_v01/rehearsal150_v10/config.json` (9e26213e…; Choice 58f); the criteria register v05; and a `SHA256SUMS.txt` over them. The text-locked draft is already in the project (header); the registration archives it, which makes its upload time tamper-evident (Choice 49h).
- **No Argoverse 2 data and no outcome is deposited.** The plan's counts and E⁰ values (S20a, S21) are outcome-free stage-A quantities; the GT inventory and the list file hold paths, producers, content classes, dates and hashes only.
- **How.** OSF's Open-Ended Registration template, with the frozen plan's sha256 and the sentence "Frozen pre-registration of the C1 Argoverse 2 confirmatory analysis; ground-truth stage B is built only after this registration." in the summary; embargo end beyond the expected publication (at most 4 years, endable early); a view-only link for IEEE Access made as soon as the registration is accepted. The guide is `40_PROTOCOL/OSF_DEPOSIT_GUIDE_v02.md`, which supersedes v01 (Choice 41b); every deposit refusal of the code names v02 (Choice 42h).
- **The record.** The lead then writes `40_PROTOCOL/C1_PLAN_DEPOSIT_RECORD_v01.txt` with the lines `frozen_plan_sha256: <64 hex>` (equal to the latest C1 line of the canonical log), `registration: <OSF registration URL or DOI>` and `registered_utc: <time OSF shows>`. It is written only after the registration exists, because its existence unlocks every AV2 outcome route.
- **How the record is validated** (the guard's `check_deposit`; Choices 42f m2, 44f m2):
  - duplicated keys are refused;
  - `registration` must match `https://osf.io/<id>/` or the OSF DOI form `10.17605/OSF.IO/<ID>` (upper case), and must **not** be the project recorded in `OSF_PROJECT_RECORD_v01.txt` (`eumj8`, as a URL or a DOI, in any case): the line names the registration, not the project;
  - `registered_utc` must be ISO 8601 UTC ending in `Z`, later than the plan's line in the hash log, no later than the time of the check, and earlier than every stage-B build in the stage-B log; the plan's hash-log line must carry a parsable time ("frozen" or "AMENDED", then `YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS UTC`), otherwise the record is refused, not the check skipped;
  - `frozen_plan_sha256` must equal the latest C1 line.
- **Which routes wait for it** (Choice 42a; R17d B1). Under this plan on AV2, every entry point that reads ground truth after k, or a table built from it, refuses to run without the validated record: stage B, items, fits, runs, method stage (H6, H7), H8, predictor arm (H9) and analysis. Only `entry:c1_e0`, `entry:c1_folds` and `entry:c1_freeze` are exempt (section 1.5). Stage B checks the record after the guard and before reading any ground truth. Every runtime record and every table carries `deposit_record_sha256`; `check_table` and the analysis's run check require it to equal the current record. `c1_transfer_apply_v01` reads only the nuScenes transfer fit and the folds and computes no AV2 quantity (section 3.2); the runs that use its output are guarded.
- **After the results:** `C1_RUN_LOG.txt` and `C1_STAGE_B_LOG.txt` are uploaded to the same project (section 8.6).
- **In the paper:** the registration's identifier and time, the text-locked draft's upload time (tamper-evident once the registration archived it), and that stage B and every table were made after the registration, as the stage-B log and the run log show. If OSF cannot take the registration (for example after 19 Feb 2027), the route goes back to the researcher (Choice 41f).

### 8.4 Ground truth: one prepared file, Rule 0 metrics, the GT inventory and the quarantine (Choices 42b, 44a, 44f m5, 45b, 46c, 46f, 47b, 48a, 49b, 49d, 49e, 51c, 52g)

- **One prepared file** (Choice 44f m5). `av2_gt_prep_v02 --logs-file 30_DATA/av2_trk_v02/C1_LOGS_375_v01.json` wrote one prepared ground-truth file covering exactly the 375 fold units, `30_DATA/av2_trk_v02/gt/av2_c1_gt375_v01.json` (e5c390aa…), before stage A (Choice 46b). Every 375-log stage-A manifest names it (with its SHA-256), and E⁰, the quarantine, stage B, the method stage and H8 use it.
- **Rule 0 metrics after the text lock** (Choice 49e). The tracker metrics (HOTA, AMOTA, MOTA, IDS) on the 375 logs are computed after the text lock and before the quarantine; when they were computed, and who read them, is recorded in the freeze-time record (Choice 51a; section 10). The detector AP (S20b) was seen before the lock (section 1.2). They are tracker and detector metrics only, never a C1 outcome. After the quarantine, no step reads plain ground truth.
- **What counts as a ground-truth file: a content rule** (Choice 49b; R20 N3 and its open point 1). A file is a ground-truth file if it holds any per-object AV2 annotation position, box, size, yaw or velocity at any frame, of any AV2 log, training logs included. That includes quantities derived per object from these (such as QCNet's per-item `p0`, per-object errors, matched-pair distances, per-object ground-truth speeds, interpolated positions) and members of archives (tar, zip, npz, pkl, feather, parquet). It does **not** include identity-only data (track ids, existence and point counts at a frame, visibility flags: the stage-A caches, the K8 count items, the QCNet join keys `join2hz_t{1,3}_v01_keys.npz`), ego poses (they come from `city_SE3_egovehicle.feather`, not from annotations), or purely aggregate files without per-object rows (AP, metric and count tables). **Not inventoried, by this rule** (Choice 49b; R17g m-8; Choice 55c): the aggregate and identity-only outputs of tools that read annotations or prepared files, such as the count parts in `60_RESULTS/av2_trk/counts_parts/`, QCNet's frame tests and tracker-history outputs, and the AV2 validation AP, reproduction and count files; none holds per-object annotation geometry. The inventory's `checked_not_listed` list does not name every one of them; that is a gap in its record of exclusions, not in the ground-truth set.
- **The GT inventory** (Choices 47b, 49b; README v10). The bound builder `c1_av2_gt_inventory_v01` writes, for every file that holds per-object AV2 annotation geometry of any AV2 log: path, bytes, SHA-256, date, content class, producer (tool, its hash now, and the hash recorded at run time where a pod record exists) and the producing code line that decides it. Archives are listed by member names (tar headers) only, never unpacked. Identity-only data, ego poses and aggregates are recorded as not listed, with reason and class, so that R17g can check each exclusion. A name sweep refuses any uncovered `*gt*`, `*infos*`, archive or pickle under `30_DATA` and `60_RESULTS`. **The current inventory is `40_PROTOCOL/C1_GT_INVENTORY_v02.json`** (4f1a9703…; written 5 Nov with `--include-port`; step 16 rebuilds it as v03, the inventory of record): **8 ground-truth files, 3,567,314,110 B, the port included, nothing pending, nothing unclassified**:
  - the prepared files `30_DATA/av2_trk_v01/gt/av2_val_gt_v01.json` (9642db5b…) and `30_DATA/av2_trk_v02/gt/av2_c1_gt375_v01.json` (e5c390aa…);
  - the detection-evaluation ground-truth feathers `30_DATA/av2_det_v01/lt3d_centerpoint/av2val_lt3d_centerpoint_gt_v01.feather` and `30_DATA/av2_det_owncp_v01/val150/av2val150_owncp_centerpoint_gt_v01.feather` (both a9d85a54…: the same bytes) and `30_DATA/av2_det_owncp_v01/held225/av2held225_owncp_centerpoint_gt_v01.feather` (8cb86154…);
  - **the two QCNet ground-truth-history files** `30_DATA/qcnet_av2_v01/qcnet_av2_gt_v01.npz` (993019f6…) and `30_DATA/qcnet_av2_v02/qcnet_av2_gt_recentred_v02.npz` (6380a729…) (QCNet on ground-truth histories, 2 Nov; each item stores the annotation centre at its origin, and origins are every 2 Hz frame, so together they hold positions after k for the 150 validation logs; R20 B2);
  - **the own detector's infos archive** `30_DATA/av2_det_owncp_v01/train_progress/infos_archive_v01b.tar` (2,838,906,880 B; SHA-256 ada2146d2fb4d61cd650b1012c7ee6ababa60243263d3e36458e99635cf835da, as the inventory gives it), classified as ground truth from its member names.

  The earlier `40_PROTOCOL/C1_GT_INVENTORY_v01.json` was port-pending (written by a draft of the tool before its `--include-port` sweep); it is the superseded draft, kept as a record and never bound. **Step 16 always rebuilds the inventory** (Choice 56d item 3; R20 re-check v03, m5), as `40_PROTOCOL/C1_GT_INVENTORY_v03.json` with `--include-port` (outcome-free; the builder refuses to overwrite), so that the tool's name sweep and producer checks run after the Rule-0 metrics and stage A, instead of a confirmation by eye. **v03 must list 0 pending entries and record `include_port` true;** the lead checks both before the quarantine (R17g m-2; Choice 55c), because the freeze and the quarantine keep only inventory entries with a hash. v03 is the inventory of record, bound as `data:gt_inventory` and uploaded. The port's list file holds only paths and hashes; it is not quarantined, and it is uploaded (Choice 46c).
- **Large archives** (Choice 49b). `c1_quarantine_v03` streams every file in 1 MiB chunks (hash, gzip at level 6, verify): an archive is quarantined whole, as one verified gzip member, never unpacked and never read into memory.
- **What is quarantined** (`c1_quarantine_v03`). The union of: **each AV2 stage-A manifest's ground-truth file**, for the manifests passed to it (the 375-log ones) and for **every** AV2 stage-A `MANIFEST.json` under `30_DATA` (to 4 folder levels), the five 150-log manifests in `30_DATA/av2_cache_stageA_v01/` included; every file of the inventory given with `--gt-inventory`; the files passed with `--extra` (the paths of the port's GT list, and any off-workspace match), recorded in the quarantine manifest as kind `extra`, with path, hash and size; and every member already recorded (Choice 49d: each manifest's ground-truth file, not every file a manifest names).
- **How.** The lead runs it with `--dry-run` first, then without. For every recorded hash, the tool hashes every workspace file of the same size, skipping `50_CODE/env/`, the quarantine folder and folders whose names contain a banned word, and treats every match alike: one gzip member in `30_DATA/av2_gt_quarantine_v01/`, verified to decompress to the recorded bytes, and only then every plain copy removed. Two files with the same bytes under different names are both quarantined (Choice 46f). A plain copy inside the quarantine folder is quarantined; any other stray entry there is refused. It writes `QUARANTINE_MANIFEST.json`. The bytes are always kept. It computes nothing.
- **Copies outside the workspace: two scans** (Choices 49b, 51c, 52g item 6). The lead runs a size-filtered hash scan for the recorded ground-truth hashes, **twice**, over three roots:
  - `C:\Users\anand\Downloads`, without the folder `ProgrammeA_RESUME_20261030_v01`;
  - this session's folder under `C:\Users\anand\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\` (`…\48b27148-cbd9-4b90-a29d-ddf2b9b9719c\`);
  - **the provenance builder's temporary folders** `C:\Users\anand\AppData\Local\Temp\c1_prov_*` (`%TEMP%\c1_prov_*`; Choice 56d item 2; R20 re-check v03, m4). Step 15's `c1_av2_provenance_v01` re-makes the ground truth, the ego file and the horizons in such folders and removes them with `ignore_errors`, so a kill, a power loss or a locked file can leave a re-made ground-truth file behind. **No `c1_prov_*` folder may remain before the freeze.** Every file in one is hashed, never opened, whatever its size. A file whose hash is a recorded ground-truth hash is a match and goes into the quarantine as below; a file that matches no recorded hash (a partial re-make, which cannot be classified without opening it) is moved and passed on `--extra` in the same way, so that nothing is deleted unexamined (Choice 57e); the emptied folder is then removed.

  The size filter is the sizes of the inventory's ground-truth files; in a `c1_prov_*` folder every file is hashed. **First at step 17, before the quarantine:** any match is moved into the workspace and passed on `--extra`, so that the quarantine records and compresses it with the others; nothing is deleted. **Again immediately before step 19's dry run:** this repeat must find no new match and no `c1_prov_*` folder. If it finds one, the freeze does not run until a D16 choice decides how to handle it. Both results, with every `c1_prov_*` folder found and what was done with each of its files, go into the freeze-time record (Choices 51a, 56d item 2; section 10). OneDrive and the other agent's folders are not scanned.
- **The freeze's check** (`c1_freeze_v07`; Choice 48a). Its hash set is the union of every quarantine member's recorded decompressed hash (from `QUARANTINE_MANIFEST.json`, of every kind), every GT-inventory file, the ground-truth hashes of the stage-A manifests it finds or is passed, and `--extra`. It refuses:
  - a plain file at a recorded path, a plain `--extra` file, or an `--extra` file not recorded in the quarantine manifest;
  - a recorded hash without a member;
  - any plain file in the scan root whose SHA-256 is in the hash set, outside the skipped folders;
  - **any file in the quarantine folder that is not a recorded member or the manifest itself**, so a plain or extracted copy beside the members refuses;
  - **a recorded stage-A member whose manifest is no longer at its recorded path**, so moving a manifest no longer opens a route.

  **The skipped folders** are `50_CODE/env/`, the quarantine folder (checked by name instead) and folders whose names contain a banned word; the freeze lists them by name in its output and in FROZEN_TESTS (R17f m-3). The banned-word skip stays, because the standing rule forbids opening such folders; its limit is stated in section 8.8. **A second line of defence:** every stage-A manifest stays in place until stage B, and the lead lists the quarantine folder (names only) just before the freeze (section 8.2).
- **Reading after the quarantine.** Stage B v06 reads ground truth only from a quarantine member, after the deposit check, and verifies the decompressed SHA-256 against the stage-A manifest. `c1_h8_v06`, `c1_mstage_v06` and the analysis's E⁰ recompute do the same, through a temporary file outside the workspace that is removed afterwards. The guard sweeps stale temporary folders (`c1_gt_<pid>_*` whose process has ended, or pid-less ones older than 10 minutes) at guard time (Choice 44f m7).
- **Outside the quarantine** (section 11.3). The public `annotations.feather` files of all 375 logs (`30_DATA/av2_val/`, `30_DATA/av2_train225/`); copies outside this workspace that the two scans do not reach (pods and their volumes, OneDrive, other machines), which section 10 records; and a ground truth rebuilt after the quarantine from the public annotations by an AV2 data tool, or a member decompressed by hand.
- **What it closes.** The one-command route of R17d's probe P2b between the freeze and the deposit, also for prepared files named only by the older 150-log manifests (R17e M-A), for the QCNet ground-truth-history files (R20 B2), for a plain copy beside a quarantine member and for a moved manifest (R17f M-1).

### 8.5 Order of operations (AV2; environments named per step; `50_CODE/env/<env>/Scripts/python.exe -B`, from `50_CODE/c1_av2` for the C1 tools)

**The port** (README v10; port design v04 section 8). Steps 1 to 8 may run before the text lock; they are builders and compute nothing on outcomes (Choice 51d). They were run by the port on 5 Nov (`60_RESULTS/av2_trk/AV2_TRACKER_NOTE_v03.md`, 0d24c2d4…). Step 9 onwards comes after the lock.
1. **Fetch** (canmot310, `-I`): `av2_fetch_v05` for the 225 held-out train logs into `30_DATA/av2_train225/` (no lidar contents; sweep lists from the recorded bucket listing through `av2_common_v02`).
2. **Log list** (canmot310): `av2_c1_logs_v01` writes `30_DATA/av2_trk_v02/C1_LOGS_375_v01.json` and its `.sha256` (`data:logs375`).
3. **Frame check** (canmot310): `av2_c1_frame_check_v01`: every log's listing-derived frame times equal the own detector's frames (7,232 held-out, 4,806 validation); a mismatch stops the port (Choice 46a).
4. **Tracker input** (canmot310): `av2_tracker_input_v02 --detector owncp --logs-file …`.
5. **Prepared ground truth** (canmot310): `av2_gt_prep_v02 --logs-file 30_DATA/av2_trk_v02/C1_LOGS_375_v01.json`, writing `30_DATA/av2_trk_v02/gt/av2_c1_gt375_v01.json`; the port's GT list by `av2_c1_gt_list_v01`.
6. **Tracking:** T1 and T3 with `av2_track_v02 --detector owncp` (canmot310; `paper/local/canmot/sc` and `paper/baseline/poly_mot`, nuScenes parameters); AB3DMOT with `av2_ab3dmot_v05 --detector owncp` (**ab3dmot310**, LT3D's defaults).
7. **Ego** (canmot310): `av2_ego_prep_v02 --gt 30_DATA/av2_trk_v02/gt/av2_c1_gt375_v01.json`.
8. **2 s propagation** (canmot310): `av2_horizon_prep_v02 --propagate-only --allow-av2-propagation` for T1 and T3 (Choice 13); AB3DMOT's own horizon files, merged in worker order.

**The text lock** (header; Choices 47i, 49a), before step 9 and before the first 375-log stage-A build.

**After the lock, to the freeze:**

9. **Rule 0 metrics:** `av2_track_metrics_v04` (canmot310) and `av2_hota_v04` (**av2eval310**), unbound and outcome-free, on the 375 logs, written to `60_RESULTS/av2_trk/c1_375/metrics/` and `hota/` (`data:rule0_metrics`). Their run times and readers go into the freeze-time record (Choices 49e, 51a).
10. **Stage A** (canmot310): `av2_cache_stageA_v02` for the 375 logs, T1, T3 and AB3DMOT; each manifest names the one prepared file.
11. **Counts:** S20a with `k8_items_v04 --av2 --counts-only` and `av2_counts_v02` (`data:s20a`), with the descriptive outputs of section 7 that need no outcome; then S22 with `c1_power_counts_v05 <out> --h9-items t1=<375-log items> t3=<…>`, its hash recorded before it runs (`data:s22`; reported only).
12. **Folds:** `c1_folds_v03 --dataset av2 --split … --val …`, then `--restrict-from` for `val` (`folds_val150`) and for `held_out_train` (`folds_train225`).
13. **Scene map:** `c1_av2_scene_map_v01 --stageA-manifests <the 375-log manifests> --out <meta>/scene.json` (`data:scene_json`). It comes before E⁰, as in README v10's command order, because `c1_e0_v05` hashes `<meta>/scene.json` among its inputs (corrected in v07i).
14. **E⁰ before the quarantine:** `c1_e0_v05 --mode c1 --seed 20261114 --workers <T1's export folders> --gt <the prepared ground-truth file> --meta <meta> --horizon <T1's horizon file> --stageA-manifests … --out …` (its `H8_E0_primary` fills S21 and the config's `e0.plan_values`; its four input arguments go into the config as `e0.inputs`, and its `inputs_sha256` as `e0.inputs_sha256`; section 6.1).
15. **Provenance** (canmot310): `c1_av2_provenance_v01 --readme 50_CODE/c1_av2/README_C1_CODE_v10.md --stageA-manifests <every AV2 stage-A manifest to bind> --logs-file 30_DATA/av2_trk_v02/C1_LOGS_375_v01.json --out <record>` (`data:provenance`, written only on success); a failure stops the freeze (Choice 46d). It re-makes files in `%TEMP%\c1_prov_*`, which the scans of section 8.4 cover (Choice 56d item 2).
16. **GT inventory, always rebuilt** (Choice 56d item 3): `c1_av2_gt_inventory_v01 --include-port --out 40_PROTOCOL/C1_GT_INVENTORY_v03.json --extra-list 40_PROTOCOL/C1_GT_EXTRA_LIST_v03.txt` (`data:gt_inventory`; outcome-free; `--extra-list` names a new file that the tool writes, the inventory's file paths one per line, kept as a record; it is never an existing name, because the tool refuses to overwrite one; Choice 58c), by the content rule of section 8.4, checked by R17g. Before step 17 the lead checks that v03 lists **0 pending entries** and records `include_port` true (R17g m-2; Choice 55c); otherwise the quarantine does not run until a D16 choice decides.
17. **Off-workspace scan, first run:** the size-filtered hash scan of the three roots of section 8.4, the `%TEMP%\c1_prov_*` folders included; any match, and every file of a `c1_prov_*` folder, moved into the workspace and added to `--extra`, and no `c1_prov_*` folder left (Choices 51c, 56d item 2).
18. **Quarantine** (canmot310): `c1_quarantine_v03 --stageA-manifests <every AV2 stage-A manifest> --gt-inventory <the inventory of step 16> --extra <the paths of the port's GT list, and any match of step 17> --dry-run`, then the same command without `--dry-run`.

**Between steps 18 and 19** (Choices 51a, 51c):
- **the repeat off-workspace scan,** immediately before step 19's dry run, over the same roots, exclusions and size filter as step 17. It must find no new match and no `c1_prov_*` folder; if it finds one, the freeze does not run until a D16 choice decides how to handle it;
- **the freeze-time record:** the lead writes `40_PROTOCOL/C1_FREEZE_TIME_RECORD_v01.md`, with the Rule-0 metrics' run times (from the run records and file times of `60_RESULTS/av2_trk/c1_375/metrics/` and `hota/`) and every person or agent that read them, both scans' results (the `c1_prov_*` folders included) and step 16's check of the inventory (section 10). The results of steps 19, 19a and 19b are added to it before step 20, and the freeze binds it as it then stands.

19. **Freeze dry run and listing** (Choice 48c): `c1_freeze_v07 --dry-run` with the arguments of step 20 except `run_commands=` (written at step 19b; the code does not require it), `--data freeze_record=40_PROTOCOL/C1_FREEZE_TIME_RECORD_v01.md` included, on the real tree (every check except the bound tests; it writes nothing). `c1_freeze_v07` refuses an AV2 freeze without `data:freeze_record`; the lead also checks in the dry run's listing that it is bound. Then the lead lists the quarantine folder (names only), and makes **two hand checks** (Choice 55c; R17g m-1, m-3), both outcome-free and recorded in the freeze-time record:
    - **the hash of every quarantine member** (Choice 58j; R20 re-check v04, m9): each member file's SHA-256 equals the `sha256_gz` that `QUARANTINE_MANIFEST.json` records for it (hashing reads the bytes; nothing is decompressed and no content is opened). The freeze checks members by name only, so this closes both the case of plain bytes and that of other content under a member's name. It replaces Choice 55(c)'s check of the gzip magic bytes;
    - **the provenance tool's hash:** the `tool_sha256` recorded in the provenance record equals README v10's hash of `c1_av2_provenance_v01`, df9b0400cdbd4df59f1f3983add9e72e494762cd3cf20a91ee25fcdb7614bc0b, which the freeze does not compare.

    A failed check stops the freeze until a D16 choice decides.

19a. **Config checks before the freeze** (Choices 55b, 56b, 57c, 58e, 58f, 59c; R17g M-2; R20 re-check v03, M-2; R20 re-check v04, m4, m5), canmot310, from `50_CODE/c1_av2`, on the config that step 20 binds; outcome-free (they read the config JSON only):
    - `python -B -c "import json, c1_rules_v02 as R; c = json.load(open(r'<config>', encoding='utf-8')); print(R.config_check(c)); print(R.check_h9_label_c1(c, 'c1'))"`, which must print both results without an AssertionError;
    - `python -B ../../70_TESTS/c1_v01/plan_v07_check/config_shape_check_v02.py --config <config> --expect-routes within_lt3d,transfer_lt3d,voxelnext_val,public_lt3d_val,source_transfer --out <log>` (27f3c463…; shape check v02, section 6.1), against its default reference, the rehearsal's configuration, which must print PASS with 0 differences and exit 0, and whose log must record `reference_sha256` 9e26213e2121facbb0b25a43d3a3654a85177c62f01da6845e13316c173f463a;
    - `python -B ../../70_TESTS/c1_v01/plan_v07_check/config_value_check_v02.py --config <config> --e0-out <step 14's E⁰ output> --folds <the file of role folds> --folds-val150 <the file of role folds_val150> --folds-train225 <the file of role folds_train225> --stageA-manifests <the seven stage-A manifests of section 8.1> --out <log>` (eeabe7e8…; value check v02, section 6.1), never with `--skip-output-absence`, which must print PASS and exit 0.

    All must pass. If one fails, the config is corrected before the freeze and step 19a is repeated in full, with a new log name. The printed results, and both check logs with their SHA-256, go into the freeze-time record.

19b. **The frozen command list** (Choice 58a; R20 re-check v04, M-1). After step 19a passes, the lead writes `40_PROTOCOL/C1_RUN_COMMANDS_v01.txt`: every command of steps 22 to 29, one per line and in the order of section 8.5, fully expanded from the config that step 20 binds and the fixed names of steps 22 to 29, by a script that reads only the config and file names (outcome-free). The script's name and SHA-256 go into the freeze-time record. The lead checks that every output path and tag in the list equals the config's entry. Step 20 binds the list as `data:run_commands`, and it is uploaded with the deposit (section 8.3). The rerun lines of step 29 differ from its first line only in their `_rN` suffix.

    **The input-binding check** (Choices 59a, 60a; R20 re-check v05, M-1; R20 re-check v06, m1), outcome-free. For every command in the list, the checked files are defined per tool: the files that each of its input options names, and, for a folder argument, the files the tool reads from that folder by its own rule (for an export worker folder: `x1_tracks.jsonl.gz`, `x1_export_record.json` and `x1_per_frame.json`). `--plan` and `--tpp-root` are excluded. Each checked file must be bound by the freeze (in README v10, or among step 20's planned `--data` arguments, which the script reads, the `mstage_train` files included), an input of a bound stage-A manifest, or the output of an earlier command in the list. A quarantine member is matched by its decompressed SHA-256 against a bound stage-A manifest's `gt_sha256`: a hash, never displayed or read as content. The lead writes the script at this step and reviews its log before the freeze; it must PASS, and its name, SHA-256 and log go into the freeze-time record.

20. **Freeze** (Choice 58d; R20 re-check v04, m3), canmot310, from the workspace root, with every option that the tool's argparse requires (`--plan`, `--readme`, `--results-dir`, `--folds`, `--split`, `--config`, `--transfer-fits`, `--stageA-manifests`) and the ones C1 needs; shown on several lines, one command:

The stage-A manifests go under `--stageA-manifests`, never among the `--data` items. The 76 `mstage_train` items are the files of section 8.1's list, one item per file (Choices 59a, 60d). **Step 20 runs from PowerShell or bash, never from `cmd.exe`,** whose command line is capped at 8191 characters (Choice 60d). No `--hash-log`, `--data-root` or `--amend` is given on AV2; `--test-out-dir`, `--tpp-python` and `--tpp-env` keep their defaults. The config holds `h8_units` = `folds`, `h9_label` = "descriptive" and no `q1`, and has passed step 19a (section 6.1). **Notation for steps 22 to 29** (Choice 58a; R20 re-check v04, M-1). Every command runs from the workspace root under canmot310 (`50_CODE/env/canmot310/Scripts/python.exe -B <script> …`) unless it names tpp38, and none takes `--hash-log`.
- `<plan>`: the frozen plan, `40_PROTOCOL/C1_AV2_CONFIRMATORY_PREREG_vNN.md`. `<cfg>`: the frozen config (role `config`); `cfg.…` is one of its entries (section 6.1), for example `cfg.routes[within_lt3d].tags.t1`.

- `<T>`: the folder `<R>/tpp` for the Trajectron++ keys and runs.

**Every output path and tag equals the frozen config's entry**, and every `--c1-folds` is the route's own folds file, `cfg.routes[r].folds`. Each guarded command is run by copying its line from `40_PROTOCOL/C1_RUN_COMMANDS_v01.txt` (step 19b). Before each run the lead checks the line's `--mode`, `--tag` and output paths against the config, and records that check in the run notes, which are uploaded with the run log.

Each is a required step. For t ∈ {t1, t3}, and also `tab` on `within_lt3d` (the C1 tools are in `50_CODE/c1_av2/`):

    transfer_lt3d:   c1_transfer_apply_v01.py --transfer-fit 60_RESULTS/c1_transfer_fits_v01/c1_transfer_fits_<t>_v01.json

    voxelnext_val:   c1_fit_v06.py --items <I:vxn150,t> --folds cfg.folds_val150 --out cfg.routes[voxelnext_val].fits.<t> --plan <plan>
    source_transfer: c1_fit_v06.py --items <I:own375,t> --route source --folds cfg.folds_train225 --apply-folds cfg.folds_val150
                       --units-subset --retuned-arm <RETUNED of t> --out cfg.routes[source_transfer].fits.<t> --plan <plan>
    every route r:   c1_run_v06.py --caches <B:set of r,t> --fits cfg.routes[r].fits.<t> --c1-folds cfg.routes[r].folds --c1-plan <plan>
                       --tag cfg.routes[r].tags.<t>_decide --mode decide --out cfg.routes[r].runs --workers 4 --resume
    ```

    - **The set of each route:** `own375` for `within_lt3d`, `transfer_lt3d` and `source_transfer`; `lt3d150` for `public_lt3d_val`; `vxn150` for `voxelnext_val`.

Without it the source fit records no RETUNED arm.
    - **`--units-subset`** is given to the source fit only: its 375-log items cover more units than `folds_train225`. The other fits' items cover exactly their folds' units.
    - **`--mode decide` is required.** `c1_run_v06`'s default is `loop`, a loop run writes files of the same names, and the analysis reads only `run_record_<tag>_decide.json` and the matching `_decide` files. `--resume` reuses shards whose hashes match, so the same line also resumes after an environment stop (section 8.6).

    A missing fit or run is a deviation (section 9), and the analysis then treats that route's input as missing, by section 6.3: NO-GO for an input of H1 (`within_lt3d`), "not tested", with the reason, for any other claim.

      --train-horizon 60_RESULTS/m_export_polymot_v01/horizon_train_v03.jsonl.gz --out cfg.method_stage.t3 --folds cfg.folds --plan <plan>

    The twelve training folders are given as worker0 to worker11, in that order. **Every `--train-*` input is bound at step 20** as `data:mstage_train` (the 76 files of section 8.1; Choices 59a, 60d): the twelve `x1_tracks.jsonl.gz`, `x1_export_record.json` and `x1_per_frame.json` of each tracker's training folders, `30_DATA/x2_gt/x2_train_gt_v03.json` (93b4ac8b…, the frozen M study's training ground truth for every tracker), `30_DATA/nuscenes/v1.0-trainval/scene.json` and the two training horizons. The nuScenes training inputs are those that the frozen M plan binds (`40_PROTOCOL/M_PREREGISTRATION_v05.md`, "Exports" and its hash list): the 700 train scenes, which are E2-AV2's training set. `--experiment` is the tracker setting of both the AV2 and the training exports, and `m_data_v04.load_split` asserts it for every export. The sizes keep their defaults (E1 B = 2000 over 20 repetitions, refit-CV 200, E2 1000); `--test` and the size options are for tests only.

    - then the forward passes, under **tpp38** (`50_CODE/env/tpp38/Scripts/python.exe -B`), for t ∈ {t1, t3, tab} and s ∈ {20261102, 20261104, 20261105}, and the ground-truth run (TPPgt) for t ∈ {t1, t3} (wrapper records compared with FROZEN_ENV_TPP):

      ```
      50_CODE/tpp/tpp_run_v03.py --tpp-root 50_CODE/third_party/Trajectron-plus-plus_eccv2020 --model int_ee --keys <T>/keys_<t>.npz
        --source trk --seed <s> --out <T>/tpp_int_ee_<t>_trk_seed<s>.npz --workers 4 --threads 1

      trk@20261105=<T>/tpp_int_ee_t1_trk_seed20261105.npz gt=<T>/tpp_int_ee_t1_gt.npz --folds cfg.folds --tracker-index 0 --out cfg.h9.t1 --plan <plan>

    T3 the same with `t3`, `--tracker-index 1` and `--out cfg.h9.t3`; AB3DMOT with `tab`, `--tracker-index 2`, no `gt=` run and `--out cfg.h9_descriptive.tab`. The `--runs` items take the form the rehearsal used (`trk=`, `trk@<seed>=`, `gt=`).
29. **Analysis** (`50_CODE/c1_av2/c1_analyse_v10.py`; B = 40000; Q1 is reported "not tested" with Choice 44(d)'s reasons; H9 "descriptive"):

    ```

    ```

    **Each run has its own CSV folder** (Choice 58g; R20 re-check v04, m6): a rerun under section 9 uses `_r2`, then `_r3` (and `_r4` after the researcher's change) in all three names, so no rerun writes into a stopped run's files, its CSV tables included. Before its decision is taken as the table of record, the lead applies section 9's resource-error rule to it: a decision whose recorded exception texts contain one of section 9's seven strings, or a run that left only one of its two outputs, is an environment stop, and the same command reruns under the next names (section 8.6).
30. **Upload** `C1_RUN_LOG.txt` and `C1_STAGE_B_LOG.txt` to the OSF project with the results.

None of these takes `--hash-log` on AV2, and no AV2 output is ever overwritten. The run log can be verified at any time with `python -B -c "import c1_guard_v05 as G; print(len(G.read_run_log()[0]))"`. **Descriptive and outside this order:** the planner sensitivities of Choice 50(g), on nuScenes development, after the text lock (section 5).

### 8.6 The run log, the stage-B log, the tables of record and resuming (Choices 42c, 42g, 44f m1, 47h, 49f)

- **Run log** (`40_PROTOCOL/C1_RUN_LOG.txt`, append-only, chained). The guard writes a **"start"** line on AV2 before the entry point reads any ground truth or table, and `run_log_append()` writes the **"done"** line with the output hashes. Every line is one JSON object (UTC, entry point, plan hash, deposit-record hash, input hashes and, on "done", output paths and hashes) whose `prev_sha256` is the SHA-256 of the previous line's bytes (64 zeros for the first). Writes are binary and fsynced, under a lock file, after the whole chain is verified. It is written by stage B, items, fits, runs, method stage, H8, E⁰ under the plan, predictor arm and analysis.
- **The analysis checks it.** The analysis records the run log's hash, line count and last-line hash at start. It requires a "done" line with the file's SHA-256 for every table it consumes (method stage, H8, H9, descriptive H9) and every route's run record. A table without one is not provenance-valid; a run record without one is a route problem; a broken chain refuses the analysis.
- **Table of record** (Choice 47h; R20 P6). For every entry point and output role, the table of record is the output of the first run with a "done" line under the current deposit record that is not an environment stop (below; section 9). Every such table is passed to the analysis. A route is "not tested" only if it has no "done" line or its run raised an exception that is not an environment stop (section 9); a table that exists but was not consumed is a deviation. **An output without a "done" line is never a table of record** (Choice 49f). The decision lists every "done" line and whether its output was consumed; the independent results review checks this against the uploaded run log.
- **An environment stop is never a table of record** (Choices 55a, 56a). A decision that records a resource error, or a run of `c1_analyse_v10` that left one of its two outputs without the other, is an environment stop although it has a "done" line or an output; the same frozen command reruns under the next output names, and the first decision free of such errors is the table of record (section 9).
- **Resuming after an environment stop** (Choice 49f; README v10, "Resuming after an environment stop"). The code renames nothing. What the frozen code allows:
  - **same-command re-runs** for the entry points whose output is written only at the end, through a temporary name and a rename: `c1_fit_v06`, `c1_mstage_v06`, `c1_h8_v06`, `c1_e0_v05`, `c1_predarm_v07` and `c1_analyse_v10`. After an environment stop that ends the process, nothing exists at their output path, so the same frozen command is not blocked. **Two cases differ for `c1_analyse_v10`** (Choices 55a, 55c m-6, 56a): if its decision records a resource error (section 9), or if one of its two outputs exists without the other (it renames `--out`, then `--decision`, one after the other), outputs exist and the same command is refused (`refuse_existing`); the same frozen command then reruns, with the same inputs and seeds, under the next output names (`_r1` for the first run, then `_r2` and `_r3`, and `_r4` after the researcher's change, each with its own `--csv-dir`; section 8.5, step 29, and section 9);
  - **resume from checkpoints:** `c1_run_v06`'s final run record is written last, so the same command with `--resume` continues from its shards; `c1_mstage_v06` resumes from its step checkpoints (`<out>.E1.ckpt.json`, `<out>.E2.ckpt.json`), which lie beside the output path;
  - **a new output name for progressive outputs:** `av2_cache_stageB_v06` (a folder filled progressively) and `k8_items_v08` (an npz written in place) leave a partial output; the same tag or path is refused, so the lead re-runs under a new tag or output name, and the partial output is not a table of record.

  In every case the run log keeps the stopped run's lines (a "start" line without a "done" line, or both lines for a decision that records a resource error), and the table of record is the first output with a "done" line that is not an environment stop under section 9. Every run's outputs, the stopped runs' included, are kept and uploaded with the results.
- **Stage-B log** (`40_PROTOCOL/C1_STAGE_B_LOG.txt`). Stage B v06 appends one line per build (UTC, plan hash, deposit-record hash, output hash), and writes only under the fixed folder `30_DATA/av2_cache_stageB_<tag>/`, never over an existing one; `--out` and a plain `--gt` are refused, and so is a second build with the same tag.
- **No overwrite.** On AV2 an existing output, or a final run record, is refused (`refuse_existing`); a rerun needs the rules above and is itself logged.
- **Upload.** Both logs are uploaded to the OSF project with the results. They show that every stage-B build and every table came after the registration.
- **Limits.** The logs record runs of the C1 entry points only, so they are no protection against the programs the claim concedes (section 11.3). Before the guard, an entry point reads only unit names or dataset tags, to find the dataset. The chain shows a changed, deleted or unchained line inside the log, and a truncated last line; it cannot show a log deleted, or truncated cleanly after a complete line, and hand-written lines with a recomputed chain pass. Re-running the frozen analysis on the frozen inputs reproduces every table; that shows the reported tables are the frozen analysis's, not that no other analysis was run.

### 8.7 How later artefacts are checked (Choices 24, 34, 36, 39, 40, 42, 44)

Everything made after the freeze is checked by provenance, not listed: each within-AV2 fit records its code, folds and plan hashes; each run record carries the plan, folds and deposit-record hashes and its folds role; the H6 to H9 tables carry their entry point, every loaded module's hash, the plan, the folds, the deposit record, their sorted unit list and its hash, and their inputs; every consumed table must have a "done" line in the run log; caches must be stage B under this plan, built by `entry:stageB` from a bound stage-A manifest and a quarantine member, with the deposit record's hash; items must chain to such caches through `entry:k8_items`; the method stage and H8 check the scene map unit by unit against the stage-A manifests. On AV2 no output is overwritten. The analysis refuses anything whose provenance does not match. Superseded entry points refuse real input (Choices 36, 39a) and fail the role checks. The whole path is rehearsed end to end on a mock frozen plan with nuScenes stand-in data (`70_TESTS/c1_v01/rehearsal150_v10/`, driver `c1_rehearsal_v09`; status `70_TESTS/c1_v01/C1_V10_TEST_STATUS.json`, all pass). The rehearsal has no run log, deposit, quarantine or AV2 data role, because nuScenes is exempt; the AV2 paths (quarantine, inventory, provenance, data roles, run log, deposit, stage B, scene map) are tested in `test_c1_v13`'s copy tree on R17d's AV2-tagged stand-in, with R17c's probes P1 to P7, R17d's PB1 and P2b, R17e's E2, E3 and E7, R17f's N1 and N2, and R20's provenance refusal as refusal tests. **The verdicts are stand-ins, not evidence.**

### 8.8 Limits of the binding (for the methods section)

- **Scope** (Choice 47d; section 11.3). The refusals are properties of the C1 entry points. Not technically barred are: the programs of the completed nuScenes studies (`x1_full_v02`, `x2_full_v02`, `m_run_v04`), bound by their own frozen plans and not edited (Choice 42b); the public AV2 annotation files of all 375 logs, which are on the analysis computer; the AV2 data tools, bound (`av2_gt_prep_v01`, `av2_gt_prep_v02`) and unbound (`av2_hota_v04`, `av2_track_metrics_v04`), which can rebuild a prepared ground-truth file from those annotations or read one; a quarantine member decompressed by hand; and copies outside the workspace.
- **Provenance records can be forged by hand.** They are JSON files written by bound entry points, and a hand-made table with correct fields, and hand-written run-log lines with a recomputed chain, would pass the checks. The pipeline refuses accidental and command-line bypasses; it does not prove the absence of deliberate forgery. The protections against that are external: the text-locked draft's OSF upload time, which the embargoed registration preserves when it archives the project's files at the freeze (Choice 49h); the registration itself before stage B; the run log and stage-B log uploaded with the results; and the independent results review, which re-derives the headline results from the raw arrays.
- **Provenance's reach.** It proves that the bound builders, on the bound inputs, write the recorded bytes. It does not prove what ran on the pod or the laptop when the files were first made. Its ego and ground-truth re-makes read AV2 annotations, so the tests use a stand-in runner for them, and the lead's run is their first real use. AB3DMOT's detection input is not checked through a receipt (its export receipts record no detection path); its tracker and its horizon are checked.
- **The quarantine depends on its order, its list and its scan.** The freeze's hash set holds the quarantine members' hashes, the GT inventory's files and the ground-truth hashes of the stage-A manifests it finds or is passed, and `--extra`. Stage-A manifests are found down to 4 folder levels below `30_DATA`; a deeper or moved manifest is not found, but a recorded member whose manifest has moved is refused. A file made after the quarantine, one the inventory missed, or a modified copy is not found; hence the content rule and R17g's independent completeness check. The scan skips `50_CODE/env/` and folders whose names contain a banned word, which the standing rule forbids opening; a copy placed in such a folder would not be found, and the freeze lists those folders by name (R17f m-3). Copies outside the workspace are scanned only in the three roots of section 8.4, twice (Choices 51c, 56d item 2); the others are recorded in section 10. The freeze checks quarantine members by name, not by content (R17g m-1); step 19's comparison of each member's SHA-256 with its recorded `sha256_gz` closes that gap (Choice 58j). The freeze and the quarantine keep only inventory entries with a hash (R17g m-2); step 16 therefore requires 0 pending entries.
- **The amendment rule's remaining limit** (Choices 44c, 48b m-4). Deleting or truncating the run log as well as deleting the deposit record would reopen `--amend` in code. What then remains is the second OSF registration and the deviation log.
- **The run log** records runs of the C1 entry points only; its chain cannot show a deleted log or a clean truncation after a complete line (section 8.6). It does not classify stops: the resource-error rule of section 9 is applied mechanically from the run log and the recorded exception texts (Choices 53, 55a, 56a; plan-only). **README v10's sentence on stops is incomplete for the analysis** (R17g M-1): it says a stopped run leaves its "start" line without a "done" line whatever the cause, but `c1_analyse_v10` records a `MemoryError` or disk-full error caught inside a route or a claim block and still writes its outputs and a "done" line. Section 9's rule covers that case; the code is not changed.
- **The temporary ground truth.** A pid-less `c1_gt_*` folder left by v07 code is swept only after 10 minutes, and a folder whose pid has been reused by a live process is kept. Such copies exist only after the deposit, so outcome-blindness is not affected.
- **The AV2 export and port builders are bound at their hashes.** The freeze compares each stage-A manifest's recorded `builder_sha256` with the bound builders, and refuses a missing or unbound one; the provenance check alone follows every stage-A input to a bound producer (Choices 46d, 48b m-2). The 375-log T1 and T3 horizons were not re-made in development; the lead's provenance run is the first. No AV2 file was opened in writing the code or this plan.
- **The scene map is the identity on AV2.** `c1_av2_scene_map_v01` writes it from the bound stage-A manifests; the per-unit checks in the freeze, the method stage and H8 confirm the unit set and names, and nothing more (Choice 44f m4).
- **One keys file.** The predictor arm needs one Trajectron++ keys file whose units are exactly the 375 fold units.
- **Trajectron++ environments** (Choices 44e, 45a). The keys and runs execute on this laptop, under canmot310 and tpp38; no pod is used (Choice 60b; section 9). On any mismatch at the predictor arm, H9's descriptive report is "not tested".
- **H4's level is selected by the route's name.** `c1_rules_v02.removal_level()` gives `H4_ROUTE` ("transfer_lt3d") 1 − 0.0025/9 and every other route 1 − 0.0125/9; the route names are fixed, and a route under another name has no folds role and is refused in a C1 run.
- **Memory at B = 40000:** about 5 GB per 375-log table on this laptop (README v10 measured a 4.9 GB peak with about 1 GB of 15.7 GB free); the analysis runs one route and tracker at a time. A `MemoryError` there is caught and recorded, so section 9's rule makes the decision an environment stop.
- **The code's register citation** (R17g m-7; Choice 55c). `c1_analyse_v10`'s docstring and every decision's `criteria.source` string name register v02. Register v05 supersedes that citation, with every value unchanged; the code's comments are not changed.
- **Q1 is not tested** (Choice 44d; section 1.3). `c1_qcnet_summary_v01` is superseded and unbound. Any QCNet result is exploratory and labelled so.
- **Unguarded by design.** `c1_transfer_apply_v01` (section 3.2); `c1_tpp_envcheck_v01`, which is outcome-free; `c1_quarantine_v03`, which reads bytes only to hash and compress them; `c1_av2_scene_map_v01`, which reads only the stage-A manifests' units and logs; `c1_av2_provenance_v01` and `c1_av2_gt_inventory_v01`, which read manifests, receipts, code, names and hashes (provenance also re-makes files outside the workspace and hashes them unopened); the Trajectron++ wrappers, which read no outcome (keys are geometry and tokens, checked against the ground truth only for consistency on a stage-B cache that already needs the deposit; runs use histories up to k); the data builders (the port's tools and `av2_gt_prep_v01`); and the Rule-0 metric tools `av2_track_metrics_v04` and `av2_hota_v04`, which the claim concedes.
- **Superseded entry points.** Under this plan every entry point other than those of section 8.1 is refused by role. README v10 lists as superseded and unbound, among others: `c1_analyse_v04`, `c1_analyse_v08` and `c1_analyse_v09`; `c1_fit_v01`, `c1_fit_v03`, `c1_fit_v04` and `c1_fit_v05`; `c1_run_v03`, `c1_run_v04` and `c1_run_v05`; `c1_mstage_v03`, `c1_mstage_v04` and `c1_mstage_v05`; `c1_h8_v03`, `c1_h8_v04` and `c1_h8_v05`; `c1_e0_v04`; `c1_freeze_v05` and `c1_freeze_v06`; `c1_quarantine_v01` and `c1_quarantine_v02`; `c1_predarm_v06`; and `c1_qcnet_summary_v01`. Earlier versions bound only as modules or sources (section 8.1) are refused as entry points by role (R20 m1). The older entry points are also refused on AV2 before the freeze for want of a plan hash log (`c1_e0_v03` runs before the freeze only on stage-A fields, outcome-free), as R17e checked (section 2, E5). The old freeze tools refuse the current README (Choice 34.7; R17e PB3 for README v07; R17f for README v08; README v10's FRZ7). The Choice 36 refusals in `c1_freeze_v01` and `c1_freeze_v02` still say "Use c1_freeze_v04", and `c1_analyse_v06 --standin` accepts an AV2 folds config but finds no AV2 run to analyse; these files are not edited again (Choice 44f m6). `c1_fit_v01` (not bound, no guard) and `c1_analyse_v04` (not bound, not edited) stay runnable, but neither gives a route to outcomes before the deposit or into the decision (README v07 m6).
- **Test reruns.** The bound suites can be rerun in the real tree after the freeze only by the freeze itself (README v07 m7).
- **Deadline.** The freeze and the registration must both happen before 19 Feb 2027, when OSF projects become read-only (Choice 41f).

## 9. Deviation policy and failure

- **After the freeze nothing in this file changes.** Between the freeze and the deposit record, and before any non-exempt run, the only route to a change is `c1_freeze_v07 --amend "<reason>"` (section 8.2), logged in `40_PROTOCOL/C1_DEVIATION_LOG_v01.md`, followed by a registration of the amended plan. `--amend` is refused once the deposit record, the stage-B log, a stage-B folder, or a run-log line from a non-exempt entry point exists (Choice 44c). After that, deviations are recorded in the same log with their reason, and the frozen analysis is reported beside any deviating one. (D19 is reserved for the GO decision report; Choice 38e.)
- **Verdict of record** (Choices 47h, 55a, 56a; R20 P5). The verdict is that of the first completed run of the frozen code under the deposit record. A run that is an environment stop under the rule below is not a completed run, whatever it wrote.
- **Environment stop against exception** (Choices 49f, 52g item 11, 53, 55a, 56a; R17g M-1, m-6; R20 re-check v03, M-1, m9).
  - An **environment stop** is a process that ends without a Python exception from the entry point: power loss, a killed process, an OS restart. The run log then shows a "start" line with no "done" line.
  - **A resource error is also an environment stop:** running out of memory or of disk space, whether the entry point raises the error or records it. `c1_analyse_v10` catches every exception that is not a guard breach inside a route and inside each claim block, records its text as "<exception class>: <message>" (in `route_problems`, `missing_inputs`, `nogo_reasons`, a claim's `exception`, a table's `why` or `E0_check.reason`), and still writes both outputs and a "done" line. **A decision any of whose recorded exception texts contains one of these seven strings is an environment stop, not a completed run**, although it has a "done" line (Choices 57d, 58h): `MemoryError`; `[Errno 28]` (ENOSPC); `[WinError 112]` (ERROR_DISK_FULL); `[WinError 39]` (ERROR_HANDLE_DISK_FULL); `[WinError 8]` (ERROR_NOT_ENOUGH_MEMORY); `[WinError 14]` (ERROR_OUTOFMEMORY); `[WinError 1455]` (ERROR_COMMITMENT_LIMIT, the paging file too small). The strings are named explicitly because CPython maps WinError 112 to errno 28 but WinError 39 to errno 22, so "errno 28" alone would miss a Windows disk-full error; v07i's statement that both Windows codes map to errno 28 was wrong for 39. **A run of `c1_analyse_v10` that left one of its two outputs without the other is also an environment stop** (R17g m-6). The classification uses these seven strings alone; it is fixed now and needs no judgement. No other exception text is treated this way.
  - **What follows** (Choices 55a, 56a, 58h, 59b; R20 re-check v04, m7; R20 re-check v05, M-2). Before a rerun the lead stops this project's other jobs on the laptop (never another agent's). The same frozen command then reruns on the same inputs and seeds under the next output names, with its own `--csv-dir` (section 8.5, step 29), and **the first decision free of such errors is the table of record**. **The ladder** (Choices 59b, 60c, which withdraw Choice 58(h)'s pod rung; R20 re-check v05, M-2; R20 re-check v06, m3):
    1. at most two reruns before the researcher's change (`_r2`, `_r3`);
    2. then **the researcher's change**, never the lead's (a system setting is the researcher's to change): for a memory error (`MemoryError`, `[WinError 8]`, `[WinError 14]` or `[WinError 1455]`), the researcher enlarges the laptop's Windows page file and pauses other heavy jobs on it; for a disk-full error (`[Errno 28]`, `[WinError 112]` or `[WinError 39]`), the researcher frees disk space instead, because a larger page file uses more disk;
    3. then one more rerun (`_r4`);
    4. if that run also records a resource error, a D16 choice decides, and the deviation is recorded in section 10 and in the deviation log.

    Every stopped run's outputs are kept, listed with the results (section 10, item 10) and uploaded, and none is ever a table of record. Within these limits a rerun is not a deviation.
  - **This laptop only** (Choices 59b, 60b). Every post-freeze step, Trajectron++'s keys and runs included, runs on no machine other than this laptop, with the workspace at its path, `C:\Users\anand\Downloads\ProgrammeA_CLAUDE_IEEEACCESS_20261030_122911`. The run records hold absolute Windows paths, and the environment checks compare versions only, so an analysis run on another machine would fail on an H1 input (on a Linux pod, for example, with `[Errno 2]`), which would be NO-GO; and `tpp_run` reads the cache from the absolute path recorded in the keys while step 28's frozen command has no `--cache`, so a Trajectron++ run made on a pod could not be read back, and H9 (descriptive) would be "not computed". This is not a choice between speed and money, so the researcher's rate policy of 5 Nov does not apply here.
  - **The other entry points** (Choice 58i, which withdraws Choice 57(e)'s extension to them; R20 re-check v04, m8). No other bound entry point records an exception in a written table: on any exception it ends without a "done" line, and the resume rules of section 8.6 apply.
  - **Why this is no fork** (Choice 56a). A seeded rerun reproduces the decision, and the rule leaves no choice of verdict. Seeded reruns of the same frozen code on the same inputs and environment are expected to give identical outputs, up to floating-point reduction order; any difference in a decision is reported (R20 re-check v03, m9).
  - An **exception** is any other exception, raised or recorded by the entry point. On an H1 input it is NO-GO (section 6.3), even if a fix exists later; on a secondary input it makes that claim "not tested".
  - **The rule is the plan's, not the code's** (Choice 53): the run-log code does not classify stops; the lead applies this rule mechanically from the run log and the recorded exception texts, and the independent results review checks the classification.
  - An output without a "done" line is never a table of record.
- **Fixes.** A code fix after the deposit is a new versioned file outside the frozen binding; its output is a deviation, logged in `C1_DEVIATION_LOG_v01.md` and reported beside the verdict of record, never replacing it. Bound files are never edited.
- **Results review.** If the independent re-derivation disagrees with the frozen code on any claim, the frozen verdict stands; the discrepancy is diagnosed and reported, and a confirmed code error is published as an erratum showing both verdicts.
- **Never allowed:** re-running with other seeds, folds, thresholds, floors, levels, B, populations, comparator sets, detectors or logs; selecting among repeated runs of the same step (the table of record is fixed by section 8.6); selecting a detector checkpoint on the confirmation logs; promoting a secondary or descriptive result (H9 included); demoting a failed primary; adding AV2 data after any outcome is seen; computing a QCNet outcome for the C1 report; letting a descriptive output (section 7) or a planner sensitivity change a frozen setting.
- **If H1 fails (NO-GO):** reported as NO-GO with H1's failed or missing conditions; a pre-specified diagnosis (M\* shown above δ in a cell, family or pooled cell; a floor; HK1c; a comparator in V_t not beaten; per tracker; within against transfer; by source; A13 fallback shares; A6 diagnostics; margins by stratum); no re-run; any method change is new work on new data. Wording: "not shown above δ, operability not shown" when Valid(M\*, t) holds on both trackers and part (ii) fails; "floor not met" when M\* is shown above δ nowhere but a floor fails; and HK1c named when it is the failed condition (section 7: on development it is the main route to a failure of a valid M\*).
- **After the run:** the independent results review (R21) re-derives H1 and the K8 tables with separate code, checks the tables of record against the uploaded run log (section 8.6), reports H1 with and without zero-SE removals (section 6.2), and computes the Monte Carlo error and seed stability of section 6.1.

5. **Code reviews and rehearsals:** R17, R17b to R17g (R17g on code v10: ready to freeze as it stands, with plan-text findings only; Choice 55), R20 and its re-checks of v07d, v07f and v07h (re-checks v01 to v03; v03 also covers v07g's changes; Choice 56) on the C1 code and plan; the criteria audit; the rehearsals on nuScenes stand-in data (verdicts not evidence). **H1-P and H5** in the final rehearsal on README v10 (`rehearsal150_v10`): H1-P's route ran and is reported descriptive; H5's route was not run there (the stand-in has no second detector), and H5 is reported "not tested" with that reason (section 1.3). On AV2 every route of the config is required (section 8.5, steps 22 to 24); a missing stage B, items, fit or run of any of them after the deposit is a deviation (section 9), listed with the results (Choice 56d item 4).
6. **The port's records** (Choices 49i, 52g item 9): the in-place corrections of `av2_track_v02` and `av2_ab3dmot_v05` on 5 Nov at 07:07 EST, before Choice 46's acceptance rule and before any 375-log output was kept, with the stopped production T1 worker of 07:06:01; and `av2_hota_v04`'s equivalence at rtol 1e-12 (section 1.1).
7. **The provenance check:** its result and output hash (section 8.1; the provenance line).
8. **The OSF project** https://osf.io/eumj8/ exists, private (`40_PROTOCOL/OSF_PROJECT_RECORD_v01.txt`). Before the freeze it holds only the text-locked draft, uploaded by the researcher before the first 375-log stage-A build; no registration is made before the freeze.
9. **The freeze-time record** (Choice 51a): `40_PROTOCOL/C1_FREEZE_TIME_RECORD_v01.md`, written by the lead after the quarantine (section 8.5 step 18) and before the freeze dry run (step 19), completed with the checks of steps 19 and 19a before the freeze (step 20), bound by the freeze as `data:freeze_record` (required on AV2), and uploaded with the deposit folder (section 8.3). It holds:
   - **the Rule-0 metrics:** their run times, taken from the run records and file times of `60_RESULTS/av2_trk/c1_375/metrics/` and `hota/`, and every person or agent that read them;
   - **the off-workspace scans:** the roots scanned and the exclusions; the size filter and the source of the hash list; the number of files hashed; every match and where it was moved; and the result of the repeat scan, which must find no new match;
   - **the `c1_prov_*` folders** (Choice 56d item 2): every one found, and what was done with each of its files;
   - **the freeze-step checks** (Choices 55b, 55c, 56b, 56d, 57c, 58a, 58e, 58j): step 16's inventory v03 with 0 pending entries and `include_port` true; step 19's hash check of every quarantine member against its `sha256_gz`, and the provenance record's `tool_sha256`; step 19a's four config checks (their printed results, and the shape and value checks' logs with their SHA-256); and step 19b's command list, with the name and SHA-256 of the script that wrote it, and the input-binding check's script, SHA-256 and log (Choice 59a).

   This text does not change when the record is written; it is not one of the reserved files (`40_PROTOCOL/C1_PLAN_HASH_LOG.txt`, `40_PROTOCOL/C1_PLAN_DEPOSIT_RECORD_v01.txt`).
10. **Not seen by anyone, to the author's knowledge:** any AV2 exceedance, margin, infeasibility, V2, TTV, NEES/ANEES or projected error. Attested and timestamped at the freeze. Every prepared ground-truth file and every file holding ground-truth positions that existed in the workspace before the freeze (item 3 and the GT inventory) was hashed and moved into a verified quarantine before the freeze; the embargoed OSF registration is made after the freeze and before any C1 step that reads ground truth after k; the run log and the stage-B log, uploaded with the results, show when each run of a C1 entry point computed an AV2 outcome. Every environment stop after the deposit, a decision that records a resource error included, is listed with the results, with its outputs, run-log lines and exception texts, and its outputs are uploaded (section 9). A resource error that persists beyond section 9's rerun ladder is a deviation, recorded here with the D16 choice that decides it (Choices 58h, 59b).

### 11.2 From v04 to v05 (kept for the record)

- **Consolidated:** v04's section 11 is folded into sections 1 to 8; nothing in this file is overridden by a later section.
- **Choice 33** replaces the GO text and Choice 31's E⁰ rule (section 6.3).
- **Choice 35** corrects v04 section 11.4: H9's population is PD on the K8 tracker-planner's constraints (section 6.4).
- **Choice 37** adds history-length strata to H9, the three history-length cells to its family, H9-noHL as descriptive, and Q1's noHL reason (sections 4, 6.4).

Also removed: v04 section 6.1 to 6.7's 150-log power and scaling tables as the plan's power (kept in v04 and in the cited JSON files), the 150-log fold rule for the primary routes, H5 as secondary, and the claim that the power tools are bound.

### 11.3 The outcome-blindness claim, its scope, and the deposit order (Choices 42a, 47d; R17d, R17e and R20 plan-text items)

**The claim, as the plan and the paper state it** (R20 P1, verbatim, adopted by D16 v26 Choice 47(d); it replaces Choice 44(b)'s text):

> The C1 pipeline's entry points refuse to compute any AV2 outcome before the freeze, and any outcome after k before the external registration is recorded. Not technically barred were: the programs of the earlier nuScenes studies; the public AV2 annotation files of all 375 logs, which were on the analysis computer; the AV2 data tools, bound and unbound, which can rebuild a prepared ground-truth file from those annotations; and copies outside the workspace. Every prepared ground-truth file and every file holding ground-truth positions that existed in the workspace before the freeze (listed with producer and date in section 10) was hashed and moved into a verified quarantine before the freeze. The embargoed OSF registration was made after the freeze and before any C1 step that reads ground truth after k (stage B, the method stage, H8 and the E⁰ recompute). The run log records runs of the C1 entry points only, so it is no protection against the programs this claim concedes. Re-running the frozen analysis on the frozen inputs reproduces every table (fixed seeds, frozen configuration); this shows that the reported tables are the frozen analysis's, not that no other analysis was run.

**Wording rule.** The words "outcome-blind boundary enforced by code" are not used, in this plan, the paper or any report on C1, without this scope.

**What the code adds to the claim** (code v08 to v10). The quarantine and the freeze cover each AV2 stage-A manifest's ground-truth file and every ground-truth file of the GT inventory, decided by the content rule (section 8.4). The freeze's hash set holds every quarantine member's hash, every inventory file, the manifests' ground-truth hashes and `--extra`; it refuses any plain copy with a recorded hash outside the skipped folders, anything in the quarantine folder that is not a recorded member, and a recorded member whose manifest has moved (`c1_freeze_v07`, Choice 48a). This closes R17d's one-command route P2b between the freeze and the deposit, also for the 150-log file named only by the older manifests (R17e M-A), for the QCNet ground-truth-history files (R20 B2) and for R17f's M-1 routes. `--amend` cannot be reopened by deleting the deposit record once a non-exempt run is logged (section 8.2). The text-locked draft on OSF dates the plan before the confirmation-set stage A (header).

**The deposit order for H6 to H8 (R17d B1).** The method-stage tables (H6a, H6b, H7) and the H8 table read ground truth after k. Under this plan on AV2 they are computed only after the deposit record exists and validates (section 8.3): `c1_mstage_v06` and `c1_h8_v06` refuse without it, read the ground truth only from the quarantine, record `deposit_record_sha256`, and write "start" and "done" lines to the run log; the analysis refuses an H6 to H8 table made under another deposit record, with another unit set, or without a "done" line. The same order holds for every other outcome route (section 8.3). Only E⁰ (outcome-free fields, before the quarantine), the folds and the freeze run before the deposit.
